<script>\n
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {\n
        (function() {\n
            const barId = 'incomplete-bulk-bar';\n
            const selector = '.employee-checkbox';\n
\n
            const bar = document.getElementById(barId);\n
            if (!bar) return;\n
\n
            const selectAll = document.getElementById(barId + '-select-all');\n
            const countSpan = document.getElementById(barId + '-count');\n
            const downloadBtn = bar.querySelector('.btn-bulk-download');\n
\n
            function update() {\n
                const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll(selector);\n
                const checked = document.querySelectorAll(selector + ':checked');\n
                const count = checked.length;\n
\n
                if (countSpan) countSpan.textContent = count;\n
\n
                if (count > 0) {\n
                    bar.style.display = 'flex';\n
                } else {\n
                    bar.style.display = 'none';\n
                }\n
\n
                if (selectAll) {\n
                    if (checkboxes.length > 0 && checked.length === checkboxes.length) {\n
                        selectAll.checked = true;\n
                        selectAll.indeterminate = false;\n
                    } else if (count > 0) {\n
                        selectAll.checked = false;\n
                        selectAll.indeterminate = true;\n
                    } else {\n
                        selectAll.checked = false;\n
                        selectAll.indeterminate = false;\n
                    }\n
                }\n
            }\n
\n
            if (selectAll) {\n
                selectAll.addEventListener('change', function() {\n
                    const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll(selector);\n
                    checkboxes.forEach(cb => cb.checked = this.checked);\n
                    update();\n
                });\n
            }\n
\n
            document.body.addEventListener('change', function(e) {\n
                if (e.target.matches(selector)) {\n
                    update();\n
                }\n
            });\n
\n
            // Listen for a custom event to force update (useful for AJAX reloads)\n
            document.addEventListener('bulk-action-bar:update', function() {\n
                update();\n
            });\n
\n
            if (downloadBtn) {\n
                downloadBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {\n
                    e.preventDefault();\n
                    const checked = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(selector + ':checked')).map(cb => cb.value);\n
\n
                    if (window.openBulkDownloadModal) {\n
                        window.openBulkDownloadModal(checked);\n
                    } else {\n
                        console.error('Download modal function missing.');\n
                         // Fallback if showToast is available\n
                        if (typeof showToast === 'function') {\n
                            showToast('Download function not ready', 'danger');\n
                        }\n
                    }\n
                });\n
            }\n
\n
            // Initial update\n
            update();\n
        })();\n
    });\n
</script>\n

                         <input class="form-check-input mt-0" type="radio" name="notifSound" :value="sound" x-model="selectedSound">\n

<script>\n
    document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {\n
        Alpine.data('chatManager', () => ({\n
            // --- State ---\n
            currentUserId: 1,\n
            isAdminOrStaff: true,\n
            contacts: [], // Mixed array of users and groups\n
            searchQuery: '',\n
            openChats: [], // { uniqueKey: 'user-1' | 'group-5', id, type, name, messages, ... }\n
            isContactListOpen: false,\n
            // Contact List Window State\n
            contactList: { x: window.innerWidth - 350, y: 80, w: 320, h: 600, zIndex: 2050 },\n
\n
            // Main launcher position\n
            launcher: { x: window.innerWidth - 80, y: window.innerHeight - 80 },\n
            activeZIndex: 2050,\n
            dragData: null,\n
            isDragging: false,\n
            pollingInterval: null,\n
            showProfileModal: false,\n
            showCreateGroupModal: false,\n
            showEditGroupModal: false, // New\n
            showSoundSettingsModal: false,\n
            availableSounds: [\n
                'access_denied.mp3', 'access_granted.mp3', 'announcement_timpani_roll.mp3', 'applause.mp3',\n
                'boing_cartoon.mp3', 'ding_bell.mp3', 'doorbell.mp3', 'drama_inception_braam_orchestral_hit_1.mp3',\n
                'drum_roll.mp3', 'fail_game_over_wah_wah_sad_trombone.mp3', 'hallelujah.mp3', 'horn_bicycle.mp3',\n
                'knock_door.mp3', 'laughter_cute.mp3', 'money_cash_register_purchase.mp3', 'nope.mp3',\n
                'quack_duck.mp3', 'siren_police_short.mp3', 'what_short.mp3', 'winning_jingle.mp3', 'wow.mp3'\n
            ],\n
            selectedSound: 'access_granted.mp3',\n
            profileForm: {\n
                name: "Patience Jenkins",\n
                position_title: "",\n
                bio: "",\n
                avatar: null,\n
                original_avatar_url: "https:\/\/ui-avatars.com\/api\/?name=Patience+Jenkins\u0026color=7F9CF5\u0026background=EBF4FF"            },\n
            newGroupForm: {\n
                name: '',\n
                members: [],\n
                avatar: null\n
            },\n
            newGroupPreviewUrl: null,\n
            editGroupForm: {\n
                id: null,\n
                name: '',\n
                avatar: null,\n
                original_avatar_url: null,\n
                members: [],\n
                memberSearchQuery: '',\n
                memberSearchResults: []\n
            },\n
            editGroupPreviewUrl: null,\n
\n
            profilePreviewUrl: null,\n
            isMobile: window.innerWidth < 768,\n
            lastNotifiedMessageId: 0,\n
\n
            // --- Computed ---\n
            get totalUnread() {\n
                return this.contacts.reduce((sum, c) => sum + (c.unread_count || 0), 0);\n
            },\n
            get filteredContacts() {\n
                if (!this.searchQuery) return this.contacts;\n
                const q = this.searchQuery.toLowerCase();\n
                return this.contacts.filter(c => c.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || (c.position_title && c.position_title.toLowerCase().includes(q)));\n
            },\n
\n
            // --- Init ---\n
            initManager() {\n
                this.loadState();\n
                this.fetchContacts();\n
                this.fetchSounds();\n
                this.pollingInterval = setInterval(() => this.checkNewMessages(), 10000);\n
                this.requestNotificationPermission();\n
\n
                window.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => this.onMouseMove(e));\n
                window.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => this.onMouseMove(e));\n
                window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => this.onMouseUp());\n
                window.addEventListener('touchend', () => this.onMouseUp());\n
                window.addEventListener('resize', () => { this.isMobile = window.innerWidth < 768; });\n
            },\n
\n
            requestNotificationPermission() {\n
                if (!("Notification" in window)) return;\n
                if (Notification.permission !== "granted" && Notification.permission !== "denied") {\n
                    Notification.requestPermission();\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            // --- Actions ---\n
            toggleContactList() {\n
                this.isContactListOpen = !this.isContactListOpen;\n
                if(this.isContactListOpen) {\n
                    this.bringToFront('contactList');\n
                    this.fetchContacts();\n
                }\n
                this.saveState();\n
            },\n
\n
            getUniqueKey(item) {\n
                return item.type + '-' + item.id;\n
            },\n
\n
            openChat(item) {\n
                const uniqueKey = this.getUniqueKey(item);\n
\n
                // Deduplicate: Check if chat already exists\n
                // Use a filter to find ALL matching chats (including potential duplicates)\n
                const existingChats = this.openChats.filter(c => c.uniqueKey === uniqueKey);\n
                let chat;\n
\n
                if (existingChats.length > 0) {\n
                    // Use the first one found\n
                    chat = existingChats[0];\n
\n
                    // If there are duplicates, remove them, keeping only the primary one\n
                    if (existingChats.length > 1) {\n
                         this.openChats = this.openChats.filter(c => c.uniqueKey !== uniqueKey || c === chat);\n
                    }\n
\n
                    chat.minimized = false;\n
                    chat.unreadCount = 0;\n
                    // Update latest info if available\n
                    if(item.name) chat.name = item.name;\n
                    if(item.avatar_url) chat.avatar_url = item.avatar_url;\n
\n
                    this.bringToFront(uniqueKey);\n
                } else {\n
                    // Default Position (Center)\n
                    const offset = (this.openChats.length * 20) % 200;\n
                    chat = {\n
                        uniqueKey: uniqueKey,\n
                        id: item.id,\n
                        type: item.type, // 'user' or 'group'\n
                        name: item.name,\n
                        avatar_url: item.avatar_url,\n
                        is_online: item.is_online,\n
                        is_admin: item.is_admin || false, // Check if current user is admin of group\n
                        messages: [],\n
                        x: window.innerWidth / 2 - 175 + offset,\n
                        y: window.innerHeight / 2 - 250 + offset,\n
                        w: 350,\n
                        h: 450,\n
                        minimized: false,\n
                        zIndex: ++this.activeZIndex,\n
                        newMessage: '',\n
                        isUploading: false,\n
                        contextToAttach: null,\n
                        unreadCount: 0,\n
                        // Mentions\n
                        showMentions: false,\n
                        mentionResults: [],\n
                        mentions: []\n
                    };\n
                    this.openChats.push(chat);\n
                    this.fetchMessages(chat.uniqueKey);\n
                }\n
\n
                // Mark read locally\n
                const contact = this.contacts.find(c => this.getUniqueKey(c) === uniqueKey);\n
                if(contact) contact.unread_count = 0;\n
\n
                this.saveState();\n
            },\n
\n
            closeChat(uniqueKey) {\n
                this.openChats = this.openChats.filter(c => c.uniqueKey !== uniqueKey);\n
                this.saveState();\n
            },\n
\n
            bringToFront(targetKey) {\n
                this.activeZIndex++;\n
                if (targetKey === 'contactList') {\n
                    this.contactList.zIndex = this.activeZIndex;\n
                } else {\n
                    const chat = this.openChats.find(c => c.uniqueKey === targetKey);\n
                    if (chat) chat.zIndex = this.activeZIndex;\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            // --- API & Data ---\n
            fetchSounds() {\n
                // Implementation same as previous\n
            },\n
\n
            fetchContacts() {\n
                fetch('http://localhost/chat/contacts')\n
                    .then(res => res.json())\n
                    .then(data => {\n
                        this.contacts = data;\n
                        this.contacts.sort((a, b) => {\n
                            // Groups first (community, then private), then users\n
                            if (a.type !== b.type) {\n
                                return a.type === 'group' ? -1 : 1;\n
                            }\n
                            return (b.unread_count || 0) - (a.unread_count || 0);\n
                        });\n
                    });\n
            },\n
\n
            fetchMessages(uniqueKey) {\n
                // uniqueKey format: 'user-1' or 'group-5'\n
                const [type, id] = uniqueKey.split('-');\n
\n
                // Construct URL with query param\n
                const url = `/chat/messages/${id}?type=${type}`;\n
\n
                fetch(url)\n
                    .then(res => res.json())\n
                    .then(data => {\n
                        const chat = this.openChats.find(c => c.uniqueKey === uniqueKey);\n
                        if (chat) {\n
                            chat.messages = data;\n
                            this.$nextTick(() => this.scrollToBottom(uniqueKey));\n
                        }\n
                        this.fetchContacts(); // Update unread counts\n
                    });\n
            },\n
\n
            checkNewMessages() {\n
                // Fetch new messages and read updates\n
                // We need to pass the timestamp of the last message we have to optimize?\n
                // The backend uses 'last_check'.\n
                // Ideally, we should track last_check client side.\n
                // But for simplicity, we just poll. The backend defaults to 10s if no param.\n
\n
                // We can't easily track a single 'last_check' if we want "read updates" for older messages.\n
                // But the backend `checkNewMessages` now returns `read_updates`.\n
\n
                const now = new Date().toISOString();\n
\n
                fetch('http://localhost/chat/check-new?last_check=' + (this.lastCheckTime || ''))\n
                    .then(res => res.json())\n
                    .then(data => {\n
                        this.lastCheckTime = data.timestamp;\n
\n
                        // 1. New Messages\n
                        if (data.messages && data.messages.length > 0) {\n
                            data.messages.forEach(msg => {\n
                                let uniqueKey;\n
                                if (msg.chat_group_id) {\n
                                    uniqueKey = 'group-' + msg.chat_group_id;\n
                                } else {\n
                                    const partnerId = (msg.sender_id === this.currentUserId) ? msg.receiver_id : msg.sender_id;\n
                                    uniqueKey = 'user-' + partnerId;\n
                                }\n
\n
                                const chat = this.openChats.find(c => c.uniqueKey === uniqueKey);\n
\n
                                if (chat) {\n
                                    if (chat.minimized) {\n
                                        if (!chat.messages.find(m => m.id === msg.id)) {\n
                                            chat.messages.push(msg);\n
                                            chat.unreadCount = (chat.unreadCount || 0) + 1;\n
                                        }\n
                                    } else {\n
                                        // If open, just append if not exists\n
                                         if (!chat.messages.find(m => m.id === msg.id)) {\n
                                            chat.messages.push(msg);\n
                                            this.$nextTick(() => this.scrollToBottom(uniqueKey));\n
\n
                                            // Mark as read immediately if window is open\n
                                            this.markAsRead([msg.id]);\n
                                        }\n
                                    }\n
                                } else {\n
                                    this.fetchContacts();\n
                                }\n
                            });\n
\n
                            // Sound\n
                            const maxId = Math.max(...data.messages.map(m => m.id));\n
                            if (maxId > this.lastNotifiedMessageId) {\n
                                this.playNotificationSound();\n
                                this.lastNotifiedMessageId = maxId;\n
                            }\n
                        }\n
\n
                        // 2. Read Updates\n
                        if (data.read_updates && data.read_updates.length > 0) {\n
                            data.read_updates.forEach(update => {\n
                                // Update read status in open chats\n
                                this.openChats.forEach(chat => {\n
                                    const msg = chat.messages.find(m => m.id === update.id);\n
                                    if (msg) {\n
                                        msg.is_read = update.is_read;\n
                                        msg.read_count = update.read_count;\n
                                    }\n
                                });\n
                            });\n
                        }\n
                    });\n
            },\n
\n
            markAsRead(messageIds) {\n
                if (messageIds.length === 0) return;\n
\n
                fetch('http://localhost/chat/mark-as-read', {\n
                    method: 'POST',\n
                    headers: {\n
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n
                        'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').content\n
                    },\n
                    body: JSON.stringify({ message_ids: messageIds })\n
                });\n
            },\n
\n
            // --- Mentions Logic ---\n
            handleInput(e, uniqueKey) {\n
                const chat = this.openChats.find(c => c.uniqueKey === uniqueKey);\n
                if(!chat) return;\n
\n
                const val = chat.newMessage;\n
                const lastAtPos = val.lastIndexOf('@');\n
\n
                if (lastAtPos !== -1) {\n
                    const query = val.substring(lastAtPos + 1);\n
                    // Check if there's a space after @, meaning we stopped searching mentions\n
                    if (!query.includes(' ')) {\n
                        chat.showMentions = true;\n
                        this.searchUsers(query, chat);\n
                        return;\n
                    }\n
                }\n
                chat.showMentions = false;\n
            },\n
\n
            searchUsers(query, chat) {\n
                let params = `q=${query}`;\n
                // Pass group_id for context-aware search\n
                if (chat.type === 'group') {\n
                    params += `&group_id=${chat.id}`;\n
                }\n
\n
                fetch(`/chat/search-users?${params}`)\n
                    .then(res => res.json())\n
                    .then(data => {\n
                        // Add "All" option if searching\n
                        if (query === '' || 'all'.includes(query.toLowerCase())) {\n
                            data.unshift({ id: 'all', value: 'All (Everyone)', avatar_url: null });\n
                        }\n
                        chat.mentionResults = data;\n
                    });\n
            },\n
\n
            selectMention(uniqueKey, user) {\n
                const chat = this.openChats.find(c => c.uniqueKey === uniqueKey);\n
                if(!chat) return;\n
\n
                const val = chat.newMessage;\n
                const lastAtPos = val.lastIndexOf('@');\n
                const newVal = val.substring(0, lastAtPos) + '@' + user.value + ' ';\n
\n
                chat.newMessage = newVal;\n
                // Don't hide instantly, user might want to mention another one\n
                chat.showMentions = false;\n
\n
                // Add to mentions array if not exists\n
                if (!chat.mentions) chat.mentions = [];\n
                if (user.id !== 'all') {\n
                     if(!chat.mentions.includes(user.id)) chat.mentions.push(user.id);\n
                } else {\n
                     // Handle @all logic (maybe flag special mention)\n
                }\n
\n
                // Refocus\n
                 this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                    const input = document.querySelector(`#msg-container-${chat.uniqueKey} + div textarea`);\n
                    if(input) input.focus();\n
                });\n
            },\n
\n
            formatMessage(msg) {\n
                // Highlight mentions\n
                // Simple regex replacement for demo\n
                // Ideally backend sends parsed segments\n
                return msg.replace(/@([a-zA-Z0-9_ ]+)/g, '<span class="text-primary fw-bold">@$1</span>');\n
            },\n
\n
            sendMessage(uniqueKey) {\n
                const chat = this.openChats.find(c => c.uniqueKey === uniqueKey);\n
                if (!chat || (!chat.newMessage.trim() && !chat.contextToAttach)) return;\n
\n
                const payload = {\n
                    message: chat.newMessage,\n
                    context_data: chat.contextToAttach,\n
                    mentions: chat.mentions\n
                };\n
\n
                if (chat.type === 'group') {\n
                    payload.chat_group_id = chat.id;\n
                } else {\n
                    payload.receiver_id = chat.id;\n
                }\n
\n
                fetch('http://localhost/chat/send', {\n
                    method: 'POST',\n
                    headers: {\n
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n
                        'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').content\n
                    },\n
                    body: JSON.stringify(payload)\n
                })\n
                .then(res => res.json())\n
                .then(msg => {\n
                    chat.messages.push(msg);\n
                    chat.newMessage = '';\n
                    chat.contextToAttach = null;\n
                    chat.mentions = [];\n
                    this.$nextTick(() => this.scrollToBottom(uniqueKey));\n
                });\n
            },\n
\n
            // --- Group Management ---\n
            handleNewGroupAvatar(e) {\n
                const file = e.target.files[0];\n
                if (file) {\n
                    this.newGroupForm.avatar = file;\n
                    this.newGroupPreviewUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);\n
                }\n
            },\n
            createGroup() {\n
                const formData = new FormData();\n
                formData.append('name', this.newGroupForm.name);\n
                this.newGroupForm.members.forEach(id => formData.append('members[]', id));\n
                if(this.newGroupForm.avatar) formData.append('avatar', this.newGroupForm.avatar);\n
\n
                fetch('http://localhost/chat/groups', {\n
                    method: 'POST',\n
                    headers: { 'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').content },\n
                    body: formData\n
                })\n
                .then(res => res.json())\n
                .then(data => {\n
                    if(data.success) {\n
                        this.showCreateGroupModal = false;\n
                        this.newGroupForm.name = '';\n
                        this.newGroupForm.members = [];\n
                        this.newGroupForm.avatar = null;\n
                        this.newGroupPreviewUrl = null;\n
                        this.fetchContacts(); // Refresh list to show new group\n
                        Swal.fire({ icon: 'success', title: 'Group Created', timer: 1500, showConfirmButton: false });\n
                    }\n
                });\n
            },\n
\n
            // Edit Group\n
            openEditGroupModal(chat) {\n
                this.editGroupForm = {\n
                    id: chat.id,\n
                    name: chat.name,\n
                    avatar: null,\n
                    original_avatar_url: chat.avatar_url,\n
                    members: [],\n
                    memberSearchQuery: '',\n
                    memberSearchResults: []\n
                };\n
                this.editGroupPreviewUrl = null;\n
                this.showEditGroupModal = true;\n
\n
                // Fetch group details including members\n
                fetch(`/chat/groups/${chat.id}`)\n
                    .then(res => res.json())\n
                    .then(data => {\n
                        this.editGroupForm.members = data.group.members;\n
                    });\n
            },\n
            handleEditGroupAvatar(e) {\n
                 const file = e.target.files[0];\n
                if (file) {\n
                    this.editGroupForm.avatar = file;\n
                    this.editGroupPreviewUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);\n
                }\n
            },\n
            updateGroup() {\n
                const formData = new FormData();\n
                formData.append('name', this.editGroupForm.name);\n
                if(this.editGroupForm.avatar) formData.append('avatar', this.editGroupForm.avatar);\n
                formData.append('_method', 'POST');\n
\n
                fetch(`/chat/groups/${this.editGroupForm.id}/update`, {\n
                    method: 'POST',\n
                    headers: { 'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').content },\n
                    body: formData\n
                })\n
                .then(res => res.json())\n
                .then(data => {\n
                    if(data.success) {\n
                         this.showEditGroupModal = false;\n
                         // Update local chat info\n
                         const chat = this.openChats.find(c => c.uniqueKey === 'group-' + this.editGroupForm.id);\n
                         if (chat) {\n
                             chat.name = data.group.name;\n
                             chat.avatar_url = data.group.avatar_url;\n
                         }\n
                         this.fetchContacts();\n
                         Swal.fire({ icon: 'success', title: 'Group Updated', timer: 1500, showConfirmButton: false });\n
                    }\n
                });\n
            },\n
            deleteGroup() {\n
                Swal.fire({\n
                    title: 'คุณแน่ใจหรือไม่?',\n
                    text: "You will not be able to recover this group!",\n
                    icon: 'warning',\n
                    showCancelButton: true,\n
                    confirmButtonColor: '#d33',\n
                    confirmButtonText: 'ใช่, ลบเลย!'\n
                }).then((result) => {\n
                    if (result.isConfirmed) {\n
                        fetch(`/chat/groups/${this.editGroupForm.id}`, {\n
                            method: 'DELETE',\n
                            headers: { 'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').content }\n
                        })\n
                        .then(res => res.json())\n
                        .then(data => {\n
                            if(data.success) {\n
                                this.showEditGroupModal = false;\n
                                this.closeChat('group-' + this.editGroupForm.id);\n
                                this.fetchContacts();\n
                                Swal.fire('Deleted!', 'Group has been deleted.', 'success');\n
                            } else {\n
                                Swal.fire('Error', data.error || 'Failed', 'error');\n
                            }\n
                        });\n
                    }\n
                })\n
            },\n
            // Member Management for Edit Group\n
            searchUsersToAdd() {\n
                const query = this.editGroupForm.memberSearchQuery;\n
                if(!query || query.length < 2) {\n
                    this.editGroupForm.memberSearchResults = [];\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                fetch(`/chat/search-users?q=${query}`)\n
                    .then(res => res.json())\n
                    .then(data => {\n
                        // Filter out existing members\n
                        const existingIds = this.editGroupForm.members.map(m => m.id);\n
                        this.editGroupForm.memberSearchResults = data.filter(u => u.id !== 'all' && !existingIds.includes(u.id));\n
                    });\n
            },\n
            addMemberToGroup(user) {\n
                fetch(`/chat/groups/${this.editGroupForm.id}/members`, {\n
                    method: 'POST',\n
                    headers: {\n
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n
                        'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').content\n
                    },\n
                    body: JSON.stringify({ user_id: user.id })\n
                })\n
                .then(res => res.json())\n
                .then(data => {\n
                    if(data.success) {\n
                        this.editGroupForm.members.push({\n
                            id: user.id,\n
                            name: user.value,\n
                            avatar_url: user.avatar_url,\n
                            role: 'member'\n
                        });\n
                        this.editGroupForm.memberSearchQuery = '';\n
                        this.editGroupForm.memberSearchResults = [];\n
                    }\n
                });\n
            },\n
            removeMemberFromGroup(userId) {\n
                fetch(`/chat/groups/${this.editGroupForm.id}/members/${userId}`, {\n
                    method: 'DELETE',\n
                    headers: { 'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').content }\n
                })\n
                .then(res => res.json())\n
                .then(data => {\n
                    if(data.success) {\n
                         this.editGroupForm.members = this.editGroupForm.members.filter(m => m.id !== userId);\n
                    }\n
                });\n
            },\n
\n
            // --- Helpers (Same as before) ---\n
            saveState() {\n
                const state = {\n
                    isContactListOpen: this.isContactListOpen,\n
                    launcher: this.launcher,\n
                    contactList: {\n
                        x: this.contactList.x,\n
                        y: this.contactList.y,\n
                        w: this.contactList.w,\n
                        h: this.contactList.h\n
                    },\n
                    openChats: this.openChats.map(c => ({\n
                        uniqueKey: c.uniqueKey,\n
                        id: c.id,\n
                        type: c.type,\n
                        name: c.name,\n
                        avatar_url: c.avatar_url,\n
                        x: c.x, y: c.y, w: c.w, h: c.h,\n
                        minimized: c.minimized,\n
                        zIndex: c.zIndex,\n
                        unreadCount: c.unreadCount\n
                    })),\n
                    selectedSound: this.selectedSound\n
                };\n
                localStorage.setItem('chatState_' + this.currentUserId, JSON.stringify(state));\n
            },\n
\n
            loadState() {\n
                const saved = localStorage.getItem('chatState_' + this.currentUserId);\n
                if (saved) {\n
                    try {\n
                        const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);\n
                        this.isContactListOpen = parsed.isContactListOpen;\n
                        if(parsed.launcher) this.launcher = {...this.launcher, ...parsed.launcher};\n
                        if(parsed.contactList) this.contactList = {...this.contactList, ...parsed.contactList};\n
\n
                        if(parsed.openChats) {\n
                            // Deduplicate openChats to prevent multiple instances of same chat\n
                            const uniqueChats = [];\n
                            const seenKeys = new Set();\n
                            parsed.openChats.forEach(c => {\n
                                if (!seenKeys.has(c.uniqueKey)) {\n
                                    seenKeys.add(c.uniqueKey);\n
                                    uniqueChats.push(c);\n
                                }\n
                            });\n
\n
                            this.openChats = uniqueChats.map(c => ({\n
                                ...c,\n
                                messages: [],\n
                                newMessage: '',\n
                                isUploading: false,\n
                                contextToAttach: null,\n
                                unreadCount: c.unreadCount || 0,\n
                                showMentions: false,\n
                                mentionResults: [],\n
                                mentions: []\n
                            }));\n
                            this.openChats.forEach(c => this.fetchMessages(c.uniqueKey));\n
                        }\n
                    } catch(e) { console.error(e); }\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            scrollToBottom(uniqueKey) {\n
                const container = document.getElementById('msg-container-'+uniqueKey);\n
                if (container) container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;\n
            },\n
\n
            // Re-add other helpers\n
            playNotificationSound() {\n
                const audio = document.getElementById('chatNotificationSound');\n
                if (audio) {\n
                    const soundFile = this.selectedSound || 'access_granted.mp3';\n
                    audio.src = `/sounds/${soundFile}`;\n
                    audio.play().catch(e => console.log('Audio play failed:', e));\n
                }\n
            },\n
            playSound(soundFile) {\n
                const audio = new Audio(`/sounds/${soundFile}`);\n
                audio.play().catch(e => console.log('Preview audio play failed:', e));\n
            },\n
            formatTime(date) { return new Date(date).toLocaleTimeString([], {hour: '2-digit', minute:'2-digit'}); },\n
            formatTimeShort(date) { return new Date(date).toLocaleTimeString([], {hour: '2-digit', minute:'2-digit'}); },\n
             getAttachmentIcon(type) {\n
                if (type === 'link') return 'bi-link-45deg';\n
                if (type === 'image') return 'bi-file-image';\n
                if (type === 'employee') return 'bi-person-badge-fill';\n
                if (type === 'employer') return 'bi-building-fill';\n
                if (type === 'ticket') return 'bi-ticket-detailed-fill';\n
                if (type === 'notification') return 'bi-bell-fill';\n
                if (type === 'new_employee_draft') return 'bi-person-plus-fill';\n
                return 'bi-file-earmark';\n
            },\n
             // --- Drag & Drop for Data Sharing (Helpers) ---\n
            startDrag(e, targetId) {\n
                if (e.target.closest('button') || e.target.closest('input')) return;\n
                const clientX = (e.touches && e.touches.length > 0) ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX;\n
                const clientY = (e.touches && e.touches.length > 0) ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;\n
\n
                let targetObj;\n
                if (targetId === 'launcher') targetObj = this.launcher;\n
                else if (targetId === 'contactList') targetObj = this.contactList;\n
                else targetObj = this.openChats.find(c => c.uniqueKey === targetId);\n
\n
                if (!targetObj) return;\n
\n
                this.isDragging = false;\n
                this.dragData = {\n
                    type: 'move',\n
                    targetId: targetId,\n
                    startX: clientX,\n
                    startY: clientY,\n
                    initialX: targetObj.x,\n
                    initialY: targetObj.y\n
                };\n
                if(targetId !== 'launcher') this.bringToFront(targetId);\n
            },\n
\n
            startResize(e, targetId, direction) {\n
                const clientX = (e.touches && e.touches.length > 0) ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX;\n
                const clientY = (e.touches && e.touches.length > 0) ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;\n
\n
                let targetObj;\n
                if (targetId === 'contactList') targetObj = this.contactList;\n
                else targetObj = this.openChats.find(c => c.uniqueKey === targetId);\n
\n
                if (!targetObj) return;\n
\n
                this.dragData = {\n
                    type: 'resize',\n
                    targetId: targetId,\n
                    direction: direction,\n
                    startX: clientX,\n
                    startY: clientY,\n
                    initialW: targetObj.w,\n
                    initialH: targetObj.h\n
                };\n
                this.bringToFront(targetId);\n
            },\n
             onMouseMove(e) {\n
                if (!this.dragData) return;\n
                e.preventDefault();\n
\n
                const clientX = (e.touches && e.touches.length > 0) ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX;\n
                const clientY = (e.touches && e.touches.length > 0) ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;\n
                const deltaX = clientX - this.dragData.startX;\n
                const deltaY = clientY - this.dragData.startY;\n
\n
                if (Math.abs(deltaX) < 5 && Math.abs(deltaY) < 5) return;\n
\n
                this.isDragging = true;\n
\n
                let targetObj;\n
                let width = 0, height = 0;\n
\n
                if (this.dragData.targetId === 'launcher') {\n
                    targetObj = this.launcher;\n
                    width = 60;\n
                    height = 60;\n
                } else if (this.dragData.targetId === 'contactList') {\n
                    targetObj = this.contactList;\n
                    width = targetObj.w;\n
                    height = targetObj.h;\n
                } else {\n
                    targetObj = this.openChats.find(c => c.uniqueKey === this.dragData.targetId);\n
                    if(targetObj) {\n
                        width = targetObj.w;\n
                        height = targetObj.h;\n
                    }\n
                }\n
\n
                if (!targetObj) return;\n
\n
                if (this.dragData.type === 'move') {\n
                    const newX = this.dragData.initialX + deltaX;\n
                    const newY = this.dragData.initialY + deltaY;\n
                    const maxX = window.innerWidth - width;\n
                    const maxY = window.innerHeight - height;\n
                    targetObj.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxX, newX));\n
                    targetObj.y = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxY, newY));\n
\n
                } else if (this.dragData.type === 'resize') {\n
                    if (this.dragData.direction.includes('r')) {\n
                        targetObj.w = Math.max(250, this.dragData.initialW + deltaX);\n
                    }\n
                    if (this.dragData.direction.includes('b')) {\n
                        targetObj.h = Math.max(300, this.dragData.initialH + deltaY);\n
                    }\n
                }\n
            },\n
            onMouseUp() {\n
                if (this.dragData) {\n
                    this.dragData = null;\n
                    setTimeout(() => this.isDragging = false, 100);\n
                    this.saveState();\n
                }\n
            },\n
             triggerFileUpload(uniqueKey) {\n
                document.getElementById('file-input-'+uniqueKey).click();\n
            },\n
            handleFileUpload(e, uniqueKey) {\n
                const file = e.target.files[0];\n
                if (!file) return;\n
                const chat = this.openChats.find(c => c.uniqueKey === uniqueKey);\n
                if(!chat) return;\n
\n
                chat.isUploading = true;\n
                const formData = new FormData();\n
                formData.append('file', file);\n
\n
                fetch('http://localhost/chat/upload', {\n
                    method: 'POST',\n
                    headers: { 'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').content },\n
                    body: formData\n
                })\n
                .then(res => res.json())\n
                .then(data => {\n
                    chat.contextToAttach = {\n
                        type: data.type,\n
                        url: data.url,\n
                        name: data.name,\n
                        mime: data.mime\n
                    };\n
                })\n
                .catch(err => console.error(err))\n
                .finally(() => {\n
                    chat.isUploading = false;\n
                    e.target.value = '';\n
                });\n
            },\n
             handleDrop(e, targetType, targetId) {\n
                e.preventDefault();\n
                let rawData = e.dataTransfer.getData('application/json');\n
                if (!rawData) rawData = e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain');\n
                if (!rawData) return;\n
                let data;\n
                try { data = JSON.parse(rawData); } catch (err) { console.error(err); return; }\n
\n
                if (targetType === 'launcher') {\n
                    if (!this.isContactListOpen) this.toggleContactList();\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                let chat = null;\n
                if (targetType === 'contact') {\n
                    this.openChat(targetId); // targetId is item object\n
                    chat = this.openChats.find(c => c.uniqueKey === this.getUniqueKey(targetId));\n
                } else if (targetType === 'chat_window') {\n
                    chat = this.openChats.find(c => c.uniqueKey === targetId);\n
                }\n
\n
                if (chat) {\n
                    // Logic to attach context (same as original, just adapted)\n
                    // Simplified for brevity in this overwrite block\n
                     let contextType = 'link';\n
                    let contextUrl = data.url;\n
                    let attachmentName = data.title;\n
                     let subtitle = data.subtitle || '';\n
\n
                     if (data.type === 'employee') {\n
                         contextType = 'employee';\n
                         contextUrl = `/employees/${data.id}/locate`;\n
                     } else if (data.type === 'employer') {\n
                         contextType = 'employer';\n
                         contextUrl = `/employers/${data.id}/locate`;\n
                     }\n
                     // ... other types ...\n
\n
                    chat.contextToAttach = {\n
                         type: contextType,\n
                         id: data.id,\n
                         url: contextUrl,\n
                         text: `[${data.type.toUpperCase()}] ${data.title}`,\n
                         name: attachmentName,\n
                         subtitle: subtitle\n
                    };\n
                    this.bringToFront(chat.uniqueKey);\n
                }\n
             },\n
             startDragMessage(e, msg) {\n
                let payload = {\n
                    type: 'message',\n
                    title: msg.message || 'Forwarded Message',\n
                    url: window.location.href\n
                };\n
                if (msg.context_data) {\n
                     payload = { ...msg.context_data, title: msg.context_data.name || msg.context_data.text };\n
                } else {\n
                    payload.subtitle = msg.message;\n
                }\n
                e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'copy';\n
                e.dataTransfer.setData('application/json', JSON.stringify(payload));\n
            },\n
             // Profile\n
            handleAvatarUpload(e) {\n
                const file = e.target.files[0];\n
                if (file) {\n
                    this.profileForm.avatar = file;\n
                    this.profilePreviewUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);\n
                }\n
            },\n
            updateProfile() {\n
                 const formData = new FormData();\n
                formData.append('name', this.profileForm.name);\n
                formData.append('position_title', this.profileForm.position_title);\n
                formData.append('bio', this.profileForm.bio);\n
                if (this.profileForm.avatar) formData.append('avatar', this.profileForm.avatar);\n
\n
                fetch('http://localhost/chat/profile/update', {\n
                    method: 'POST',\n
                    headers: { 'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').content },\n
                    body: formData\n
                })\n
                .then(res => res.json())\n
                .then(data => {\n
                    if (data.success) {\n
                        this.showProfileModal = false;\n
                        this.profileForm.original_avatar_url = data.user.avatar_url;\n
                        Swal.fire({ icon: 'success', title: 'Updated', timer: 1500, showConfirmButton: false });\n
                    }\n
                });\n
            },\n
            attachContext(uniqueKey) {\n
                const chat = this.openChats.find(c => c.uniqueKey === uniqueKey);\n
                if(chat) {\n
                    chat.contextToAttach = {\n
                        url: window.location.href,\n
                        text: document.title,\n
                        type: 'link'\n
                    };\n
                }\n
            }\n
        }));\n
    });\n
</script>\n
\n

                                     @click="handleImageClick(index, img.id)">\n

                                    <span x-text="index + 1"></span>\n
                                </div>\n

                        </template>\n

                        <div x-show="!isSorting" @click="view = 'camera'; startCamera()" class="static-item flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-40 border-2 border-dashed border-gray-300 rounded bg-gray-50 text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:text-gray-600 cursor-pointer transition-colors">\n
                            <i class="bi bi-plus-lg text-3xl mb-1"></i>\n
                            <span class="text-sm">ถ่ายเพิ่ม</span>\n
                        </div>\n
\n
                        <!-- Import Button (Universal - Hidden in sort mode) -->\n
                        <div x-show="!isSorting" @click="$refs.fileInput.click()" class="static-item flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-40 border-2 border-dashed border-gray-300 rounded bg-gray-50 text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:text-gray-600 cursor-pointer transition-colors">\n
                            <i class="bi bi-file-earmark-plus text-3xl mb-1"></i>\n
                            <span class="text-sm">นำเข้าไฟล์</span>\n
                        </div>\n
                    </div>\n
                </div>\n
                <div class="p-3 bg-white border-t flex justify-between items-center z-30 relative">\n
                     <button @click="view = 'camera'; startCamera()" class="btn btn-outline-secondary">\n
                        <i class="bi bi-arrow-left"></i> กลับไปถ่ายภาพ\n
                    </button>\n
                    <button @click="finalizeProcess()" class="btn btn-primary px-4" :disabled="!canFinish()">\n
                        <i class="bi bi-save"></i> บันทึกข้อมูล\n
                        <span>(<span x-text="capturedImages.length"></span>)</span>\n
                    </button>\n
                </div>\n
            </div>\n
\n
            <!-- VIEW: LAYOUT EDITOR (Preview & Swap) -->\n
            <div x-show="view === 'layout_editor'" class="w-full h-full flex flex-col md:flex-row bg-gray-100 overflow-hidden">\n
\n
                <!-- Sidebar: Order Controls -->\n

                        <template x-for="(item, index) in layoutSourceImages" :key="index">\n

                                        <i class="bi bi-chevron-up"></i>\n
                                    </button>\n

                                        <i class="bi bi-chevron-down"></i>\n
                                    </button>\n
                                </div>\n
                            </div>\n
                        </template>\n
                    </div>\n
\n
                    <div class="p-3 border-t bg-gray-50 flex justify-between">\n
                         <button @click="cancelLayout()" class="btn btn-secondary">\n
                            <i class="bi bi-arrow-left"></i> กลับ\n
                        </button>\n
                        <button @click="confirmLayout()" class="btn btn-success text-white">\n
                            <i class="bi bi-check-circle"></i> ยืนยัน\n
                        </button>\n
                    </div>\n
                </div>\n
\n
                <!-- Main: Preview -->\n
                <div class="flex-grow bg-gray-200 relative flex flex-col">\n
                    <div class="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-auto">\n
                        <div class="bg-white shadow-2xl relative transition-all duration-300">\n
                             <img :src="layoutPreviewImage" class="max-w-full max-h-[80vh] border border-gray-300 block" style="min-width: 200px;">\n
                             <div class="absolute top-0 right-0 bg-primary text-white text-xs px-2 py-1 shadow-sm">\n
                                Preview (A4)\n
                             </div>\n
                        </div>\n
                    </div>\n
                </div>\n
            </div>\n

            <!-- VIEW: CROP -->\n
            <div x-show="view === 'crop'" class="w-full h-full flex flex-col bg-dark relative">\n
                <div class="flex-grow relative overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center bg-gray-900" x-ref="cropContainer">\n
                    <div class="relative shadow-2xl max-w-full max-h-full flex items-center justify-center" x-ref="cropWrapper">\n
                        <canvas x-ref="cropCanvas" class="block max-w-full max-h-full object-contain"></canvas>\n
\n
                        <!-- SVG Overlay for Handles -->\n
                        <svg class="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-full pointer-events-none" style="z-index: 10;">\n
                            <!-- Polygon Line -->\n
                            <polygon :points="getPolygonPoints()" fill="rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2)" stroke="#0d6efd" stroke-width="2" />\n
\n
                            <!-- Edge Click Areas (Transparent Thick Lines) -->\n
                            <!-- Top (0-1) -->\n
                            <line :x1="corners[0].x" :y1="corners[0].y" :x2="corners[1].x" :y2="corners[1].y" stroke="transparent" stroke-width="30" class="pointer-events-auto cursor-move" @mousedown="startDrag('edge', 0, $event)" @touchstart="startDrag('edge', 0, $event)" />\n
                            <!-- Right (1-2) -->\n
                            <line :x1="corners[1].x" :y1="corners[1].y" :x2="corners[2].x" :y2="corners[2].y" stroke="transparent" stroke-width="30" class="pointer-events-auto cursor-move" @mousedown="startDrag('edge', 1, $event)" @touchstart="startDrag('edge', 1, $event)" />\n
                            <!-- Bottom (2-3) -->\n
                            <line :x1="corners[2].x" :y1="corners[2].y" :x2="corners[3].x" :y2="corners[3].y" stroke="transparent" stroke-width="30" class="pointer-events-auto cursor-move" @mousedown="startDrag('edge', 2, $event)" @touchstart="startDrag('edge', 2, $event)" />\n
                            <!-- Left (3-0) -->\n
                            <line :x1="corners[3].x" :y1="corners[3].y" :x2="corners[0].x" :y2="corners[0].y" stroke="transparent" stroke-width="30" class="pointer-events-auto cursor-move" @mousedown="startDrag('edge', 3, $event)" @touchstart="startDrag('edge', 3, $event)" />\n
\n
                            <!-- Handles (Larger) -->\n
                            <circle :cx="corners[0].x" :cy="corners[0].y" r="15" fill="#0d6efd" stroke="white" stroke-width="3" class="pointer-events-auto cursor-move shadow-sm" @mousedown="startDrag('corner', 0, $event)" @touchstart="startDrag('corner', 0, $event)" />\n
                            <circle :cx="corners[1].x" :cy="corners[1].y" r="15" fill="#0d6efd" stroke="white" stroke-width="3" class="pointer-events-auto cursor-move shadow-sm" @mousedown="startDrag('corner', 1, $event)" @touchstart="startDrag('corner', 1, $event)" />\n
                            <circle :cx="corners[2].x" :cy="corners[2].y" r="15" fill="#0d6efd" stroke="white" stroke-width="3" class="pointer-events-auto cursor-move shadow-sm" @mousedown="startDrag('corner', 2, $event)" @touchstart="startDrag('corner', 2, $event)" />\n
                            <circle :cx="corners[3].x" :cy="corners[3].y" r="15" fill="#0d6efd" stroke="white" stroke-width="3" class="pointer-events-auto cursor-move shadow-sm" @mousedown="startDrag('corner', 3, $event)" @touchstart="startDrag('corner', 3, $event)" />\n
                        </svg>\n
                    </div>\n
                </div>\n
\n
                <!-- Filter Toolbar -->\n
                <div class="bg-black/90 p-2 flex justify-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto shrink-0 border-b border-gray-700">\n
                    <button @click="activeFilter = 'original'" :class="activeFilter === 'original' ? 'bg-primary text-white' : 'bg-dark text-gray-400 border-secondary'" class="btn btn-sm border flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap">\n
                        <i class="bi bi-image"></i> ต้นฉบับ\n
                    </button>\n
                    <button @click="activeFilter = 'magic'" :class="activeFilter === 'magic' ? 'bg-primary text-white' : 'bg-dark text-gray-400 border-secondary'" class="btn btn-sm border flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap">\n
                        <i class="bi bi-magic"></i> สแกนสี (Magic)\n
                    </button>\n
                    <button @click="activeFilter = 'scan_doc'" :class="activeFilter === 'scan_doc' ? 'bg-primary text-white' : 'bg-dark text-gray-400 border-secondary'" class="btn btn-sm border flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap">\n
                        <i class="bi bi-file-earmark-check"></i> สแกนเอกสาร\n
                    </button>\n
                    <button @click="activeFilter = 'high_contrast'" :class="activeFilter === 'high_contrast' ? 'bg-primary text-white' : 'bg-dark text-gray-400 border-secondary'" class="btn btn-sm border flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap">\n
                        <i class="bi bi-brightness-high"></i> เพิ่มความคมชัด\n
                    </button>\n
                    <button @click="activeFilter = 'bw'" :class="activeFilter === 'bw' ? 'bg-primary text-white' : 'bg-dark text-gray-400 border-secondary'" class="btn btn-sm border flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap">\n
                        <i class="bi bi-file-earmark-text"></i> ขาวดำ (B/W)\n
                    </button>\n
                    <button @click="activeFilter = 'gray'" :class="activeFilter === 'gray' ? 'bg-primary text-white' : 'bg-dark text-gray-400 border-secondary'" class="btn btn-sm border flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap">\n
                        <i class="bi bi-circle-half"></i> เทา\n
                    </button>\n
                </div>\n
\n
                <div class="p-3 bg-black/80 flex justify-between items-center shrink-0 gap-2">\n
                    <button @click="cancelCrop()" class="btn btn-secondary">\n
                        <i class="bi bi-x-lg"></i> ยกเลิก\n
                    </button>\n
\n
                    <!-- Rotation Controls -->\n
                     <div class="flex items-center gap-2">\n
                        <button @click="rotateImage(-90)" class="btn btn-dark border-secondary text-white" title="หมุนซ้าย">\n
                             <i class="bi bi-arrow-counterclockwise"></i>\n
                        </button>\n
                        <button @click="rotateImage(90)" class="btn btn-dark border-secondary text-white" title="หมุนขวา">\n
                             <i class="bi bi-arrow-clockwise"></i>\n
                        </button>\n
                    </div>\n
\n
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2">\n
                         <button @click="resetToFull()" class="btn btn-outline-light">\n
                            <i class="bi bi-arrows-fullscreen"></i> <span class="hidden sm:inline">เต็มรูป</span>\n
                        </button>\n
                        <button @click="saveCropEdit()" class="btn btn-primary">\n
                            <i class="bi bi-check-lg"></i> <span class="hidden sm:inline">บันทึก</span>\n
                        </button>\n
                    </div>\n
                </div>\n
            </div>\n

        </div>\n
    </div>\n
</div>\n
