<!-- Load Libraries (CDN) -->\n
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/jspdf/2.5.1/jspdf.umd.min.js"></script>\n
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.min.js"></script>\n
<script async src="https://docs.opencv.org/4.8.0/opencv.js" onload="document.dispatchEvent(new Event('opencv-loaded'))"></script>\n
\n
<script>\n
    // Global Interceptor Function\n
    window.interceptFileSelect = function(event) {\n
        const input = event.target;\n
        const files = input.files;\n
\n
        // 1. Check if this change was triggered by the scanner itself\n
        if (input.dataset.scannerSource === 'true') {\n
            delete input.dataset.scannerSource; // Reset flag\n
            return; // Allow normal processing\n
        }\n
\n
        if (!files || files.length === 0) return;\n
\n
        // 2. Check if files are supported (Image or PDF)\n
        let isSupported = true;\n
        for (let i = 0; i < files.length; i++) {\n
            const type = files[i].type;\n
            if (!type.startsWith('image/') && type !== 'application/pdf') {\n
                isSupported = false;\n
                break;\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        // 3. If supported, intercept and open scanner\n
        if (isSupported) {\n
            // Stop other listeners (e.g. immediate upload)\n
            event.stopImmediatePropagation();\n
            // event.preventDefault(); // change event is not cancellable usually, but good practice\n
\n
            // Dispatch event with files\n
            document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('open-document-scanner', {\n
                detail: {\n
                    inputId: input.id,\n
                    files: files\n
                }\n
            }));\n
\n
            // Clear the input so it's "empty" while scanning\n
            // Note: This might not be strictly necessary if we stopped propagation,\n
            // but keeps UI clean.\n
            input.value = '';\n
        }\n
    };\n
\n
    document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {\n
        Alpine.data('documentScanner', () => ({\n
            isOpen: false,\n
            isLoading: false,\n
            isProcessing: false,\n
            loadingMessage: '',\n
            view: 'camera', // camera, review, crop\n
            scanMode: 'document', // 'document' or 'id_card'\n
            targetInputId: null,\n
            targetPreviewId: null,\n
            flash: false,\n
\n
            // Reordering State\n
            isSorting: false,\n
            sortOrder: [], // Array of IDs in selected order\n
\n
            // Camera\n
            stream: null,\n
\n
            // Data\n
            // Structure: { id, original: dataUrl, cropped: dataUrl, corners: [{x,y}...] }\n
            capturedImages: [],\n
            selectedIndices: [], // Indices of selected images for layout\n
            currentEditIndex: -1,\n
\n
            // Layout Editor State\n
            layoutSourceImages: [], // { src, originalIndex }\n
            layoutPreviewImage: null,\n
            layoutType: null,\n
\n
            // Cropping State\n
            cvLoaded: false,\n
            corners: [{x:0, y:0}, {x:0, y:0}, {x:0, y:0}, {x:0, y:0}],\n
            canvasWidth: 0,\n
            canvasHeight: 0,\n
            imageWidth: 0,\n
            imageHeight: 0,\n
            scaleX: 1,\n
            scaleY: 1,\n
            activeDragIndex: -1,\n
            activeDragEdge: -1,\n
            previousMousePosition: { x: 0, y: 0 },\n
            rotation: 0, // Current rotation in degrees (0, 90, 180, 270)\n
            activeFilter: 'original', // original, bw, magic, gray\n
            editorSourceCanvas: null, // Store rotated source for preview\n
\n
            init() {\n
                // Initialize PDF.js worker\n
                if (typeof pdfjsLib !== 'undefined') {\n
                    pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js';\n
                }\n
\n
                // Fix Race Condition: Check if OpenCV is already loaded\n
                if (typeof cv !== 'undefined' && cv.Mat) {\n
                    this.cvLoaded = true;\n
                    console.log('OpenCV Already Loaded');\n
                }\n
\n
                document.addEventListener('opencv-loaded', () => {\n
                    this.cvLoaded = true;\n
                    console.log('OpenCV Loaded');\n
                });\n
\n
                // Mouse/Touch Move Handlers for Cropping\n
                window.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => this.onDrag(e));\n
                window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => this.stopDrag());\n
                window.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => this.onDrag(e), {passive: false});\n
                window.addEventListener('touchend', () => this.stopDrag());\n
\n
                // Watch activeFilter for live preview\n
                this.$watch('activeFilter', () => {\n
                     if (this.view === 'crop') {\n
                         this.renderEditorCanvas();\n
                     }\n
                });\n
            },\n
\n
            getHeaderTitle() {\n
                if(this.view === 'camera') return this.scanMode === 'id_card' ? 'สแกนบัตรประชาชน (Scan ID Card)' : 'สแกนเอกสารทั่วไป (Scan Document)';\n
                if(this.view === 'review') return 'ตรวจสอบเอกสาร';\n
                if(this.view === 'crop') return 'ปรับมุมเอกสาร';\n
                return 'Document Scanner';\n
            },\n
\n
            setMode(mode) {\n
                // Simplified Mode Switching - No data loss\n
                this.scanMode = mode;\n
            },\n
\n
            async openScanner(detail) {\n
                console.log('Opening Scanner. Files:', detail.files ? detail.files.length : 0, 'URL:', detail.initialUrl);\n
                this.targetInputId = detail.inputId;\n
                this.targetPreviewId = detail.previewId || null;\n
                this.isOpen = true;\n
                this.capturedImages = [];\n
                this.view = 'camera';\n
                this.scanMode = 'document'; // Default\n
\n
                // Handle Edit Mode (Initial File or Files Object)\n
                if (detail.files && detail.files.length > 0) {\n
                     console.log('Importing files directly');\n
                     await this.handleImport({ target: { files: detail.files, value: '' } }, true); // Pass true to indicate direct file loading\n
                } else if (detail.initialUrl) {\n
                    await this.loadInitialFile(detail.initialUrl);\n
                }\n
\n
                const fileCount = detail.files ? detail.files.length : 0;\n
                const hasFiles = fileCount > 0;\n
                const hasInitialContent = !!detail.initialUrl || hasFiles;\n
                console.log('Debug Calc:', { url: detail.initialUrl, fileCount, hasFiles, hasInitialContent });\n
\n
                if(!this.cvLoaded) {\n
                     this.isLoading = true;\n
                     this.loadingMessage = 'กำลังโหลดระบบประมวลผลภาพ...';\n
                     // Check regularly if loaded\n
                     let checkInterval = setInterval(() => {\n
                         if(typeof cv !== 'undefined') {\n
                             this.cvLoaded = true;\n
                             this.isLoading = false;\n
                             clearInterval(checkInterval);\n
                             console.log('Interval check. hasInitialContent:', hasInitialContent);\n
                             if (!hasInitialContent) this.startCamera();\n
                         }\n
                     }, 500);\n
\n
                     // Fallback timeout\n
                     setTimeout(() => {\n
                         if(!this.cvLoaded && this.isLoading) {\n
                             this.isLoading = false;\n
                             alert('Cannot load Image Processing Engine (OpenCV). Basic features only.');\n
                             if (!hasInitialContent) this.startCamera();\n
                         }\n
                     }, 10000);\n
                } else {\n
                    if (!hasInitialContent) this.startCamera();\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            async loadInitialFile(url) {\n
                this.isLoading = true;\n
                this.loadingMessage = 'Loading file...';\n
                try {\n
                    const response = await fetch(url);\n
                    const blob = await response.blob();\n
                    const mimeType = blob.type;\n
                    const filename = url.split('/').pop() || 'file';\n
                    const file = new File([blob], filename, { type: mimeType });\n
\n
                    if (mimeType.startsWith('image/')) {\n
                        await this.processImageFile(file);\n
                    } else if (mimeType === 'application/pdf') {\n
                        await this.processPdfFile(file);\n
                    } else {\n
                         // Try image as fallback if mime is generic binary\n
                         await this.processImageFile(file);\n
                    }\n
                    this.view = 'review';\n
                } catch (e) {\n
                    console.error("Load Initial File Error", e);\n
                    alert("Cannot load file for editing: " + e.message);\n
                } finally {\n
                    this.isLoading = false;\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            closeScanner() {\n
                this.stopCamera();\n
                this.isOpen = false;\n
            },\n
\n
            async handleImport(event, directLoad = false) {\n
                const files = event.target.files;\n
                if (!files || files.length === 0) return;\n
\n
                this.isLoading = true;\n
                this.loadingMessage = 'กำลังนำเข้าไฟล์...';\n
\n
                try {\n
                    for (let i = 0; i < files.length; i++) {\n
                        const file = files[i];\n
\n
                        if (file.type.startsWith('image/')) {\n
                            await this.processImageFile(file);\n
                        } else if (file.type === 'application/pdf') {\n
                            await this.processPdfFile(file);\n
                        }\n
                    }\n
\n
                    this.stopCamera();\n
                    this.view = 'review';\n
\n
                    // Reset input only if event came from actual input\n
                    if (!directLoad && event.target) {\n
                        event.target.value = '';\n
                    }\n
                } catch (err) {\n
                    console.error("Import Error:", err);\n
                    alert("เกิดข้อผิดพลาดในการนำเข้าไฟล์: " + err.message);\n
                } finally {\n
                    this.isLoading = false;\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            async processImageFile(file) {\n
                return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n
                    const reader = new FileReader();\n
                    reader.onload = async (e) => {\n
                        const dataUrl = e.target.result;\n
                        const img = await this.loadImage(dataUrl);\n
\n
                        this.capturedImages.push({\n
                            id: Date.now() + Math.random(),\n
                            original: dataUrl,\n
                            cropped: dataUrl,\n
                            corners: this.getDefaultCorners(img.width, img.height),\n
                            isFound: false\n
                        });\n
                        resolve();\n
                    };\n
                    reader.onerror = reject;\n
                    reader.readAsDataURL(file);\n
                });\n
            },\n
\n
            async processPdfFile(file) {\n
                if (typeof pdfjsLib === 'undefined') {\n
                    throw new Error('ระบบยังไม่พร้อมสำหรับการนำเข้า PDF กรุณารอสักครู่หรือรีเฟรชหน้าเว็บ');\n
                }\n
\n
                const arrayBuffer = await file.arrayBuffer();\n
                const loadingTask = pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: arrayBuffer });\n
                const pdf = await loadingTask.promise;\n
\n
                for (let pageNum = 1; pageNum <= pdf.numPages; pageNum++) {\n
                    this.loadingMessage = `กำลังแปลง PDF หน้าที่ ${pageNum}/${pdf.numPages}...`;\n
\n
                    const page = await pdf.getPage(pageNum);\n
                    const viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: 2.0 }); // High quality\n
\n
                    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n
                    const context = canvas.getContext('2d');\n
                    canvas.height = viewport.height;\n
                    canvas.width = viewport.width;\n
\n
                    await page.render({ canvasContext: context, viewport: viewport }).promise;\n
\n
                    const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.9);\n
\n
                    this.capturedImages.push({\n
                        id: Date.now() + Math.random(),\n
                        original: dataUrl,\n
                        cropped: dataUrl,\n
                        corners: this.getDefaultCorners(canvas.width, canvas.height),\n
                        isFound: false\n
                    });\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            async startCamera() {\n
                try {\n
                    this.stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({\n
                        video: {\n
                            facingMode: 'environment',\n
                            width: { ideal: 1920 },\n
                            height: { ideal: 1080 }\n
                        },\n
                        audio: false\n
                    });\n
                    this.$refs.video.srcObject = this.stream;\n
                } catch (err) {\n
                    console.error("Camera Error:", err);\n
                    alert("Cannot access camera: " + err.message);\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            stopCamera() {\n
                if (this.stream) {\n
                    this.stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());\n
                    this.stream = null;\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            canFinish() {\n
                if (this.capturedImages.length === 0) return false;\n
                if (this.scanMode === 'id_card' && this.capturedImages.length < 2) return false;\n
                return true;\n
            },\n
\n
            // --- SMART CAPTURE LOGIC ---\n
\n
            captureImage() {\n
                if (this.isProcessing) return;\n
                // Removed ID Card Limit Check to allow flexible scanning\n
\n
                this.isProcessing = true;\n
                this.flash = true;\n
                setTimeout(() => this.flash = false, 150);\n
\n
                const video = this.$refs.video;\n
                const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n
                canvas.width = video.videoWidth;\n
                canvas.height = video.videoHeight;\n
                const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n
                ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0);\n
\n
                const originalDataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.9);\n
\n
                // PROCESS IMAGE\n
                try {\n
                    if (typeof cv !== 'undefined') {\n
                        // 1. Detect Edges (SMARTER)\n
                        const imgData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n
                        const src = cv.matFromImageData(imgData);\n
                        const { corners, found } = this.detectDocument(src);\n
\n
                        // 2. Warp (Crop)\n
                        const croppedDataUrl = this.performWarp(src, corners, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n
\n
                        // 3. Store\n
                        this.capturedImages.push({\n
                            id: Date.now(),\n
                            original: originalDataUrl,\n
                            cropped: croppedDataUrl,\n
                            corners: corners, // Store scaled to original image\n
                            isFound: found\n
                        });\n
\n
                        src.delete();\n
                    } else {\n
                        // Fallback\n
                        this.capturedImages.push({\n
                            id: Date.now(),\n
                            original: originalDataUrl,\n
                            cropped: originalDataUrl,\n
                            corners: this.getDefaultCorners(canvas.width, canvas.height),\n
                            isFound: false\n
                        });\n
                    }\n
\n
                    // Auto-advance for ID card\n
                    if(this.scanMode === 'id_card' && this.capturedImages.length === 2) {\n
                        setTimeout(() => {\n
                            this.finishCapture();\n
                        }, 500);\n
                    }\n
\n
                } catch (e) {\n
                    console.error("Capture Processing Error:", e);\n
                    // Fallback on error\n
                    this.capturedImages.push({\n
                        id: Date.now(),\n
                        original: originalDataUrl,\n
                        cropped: originalDataUrl,\n
                        corners: this.getDefaultCorners(canvas.width, canvas.height),\n
                        isFound: false\n
                    });\n
                } finally {\n
                    this.isProcessing = false;\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            detectDocument(src) {\n
                const width = src.cols;\n
                const height = src.rows;\n
                let bestCorners = [];\n
                let isFound = false;\n
\n
                // Declare Mats outside for cleanup\n
                let processingSrc = null;\n
                let gray = null;\n
                let blurred = null;\n
                let edges = null;\n
                let dilated = null;\n
                let kernel = null;\n
                let contours = null;\n
                let hierarchy = null;\n
                let approx = null;\n
\n
                try {\n
                    // 1. Preprocessing (Resize for speed & noise reduction)\n
                    processingSrc = src; // Default alias\n
                    let scale = 1;\n
                    const maxDim = 800;\n
\n
                    if (Math.max(width, height) > maxDim) {\n
                        scale = maxDim / Math.max(width, height);\n
                        processingSrc = new cv.Mat();\n
                        cv.resize(src, processingSrc, new cv.Size(0, 0), scale, scale, cv.INTER_AREA);\n
                    }\n
\n
                    const pWidth = processingSrc.cols;\n
                    const pHeight = processingSrc.rows;\n
\n
                    gray = new cv.Mat();\n
                    cv.cvtColor(processingSrc, gray, cv.COLOR_RGBA2GRAY, 0);\n
\n
                    // 2. Denoise (Gaussian Blur)\n
                    blurred = new cv.Mat();\n
                    cv.GaussianBlur(gray, blurred, new cv.Size(5, 5), 0, 0, cv.BORDER_DEFAULT);\n
\n
                    // 3. Edge Detection (Canny is generally more structural than simple thresholding)\n
                    edges = new cv.Mat();\n
                    // Thresholds: Lower 75, Upper 200 is a standard starting point\n
                    cv.Canny(blurred, edges, 75, 200);\n
\n
                    // 4. Dilate to connect broken edges\n
                    kernel = cv.Mat.ones(3, 3, cv.CV_8U);\n
                    dilated = new cv.Mat();\n
                    cv.dilate(edges, dilated, kernel);\n
\n
                    // 5. Find Contours\n
                    contours = new cv.MatVector();\n
                    hierarchy = new cv.Mat();\n
                    cv.findContours(dilated, contours, hierarchy, cv.RETR_EXTERNAL, cv.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE);\n
\n
                    // 6. Find Best Quadrilateral\n
                    let maxArea = 0;\n
                    let bestCandidate = null;\n
                    const minArea = pWidth * pHeight * 0.10; // Must be at least 10%\n
\n
                    for (let i = 0; i < contours.size(); ++i) {\n
                        const cnt = contours.get(i);\n
                        const area = cv.contourArea(cnt);\n
\n
                        if (area < minArea) continue;\n
\n
                        let peri = cv.arcLength(cnt, true);\n
                        approx = new cv.Mat();\n
                        cv.approxPolyDP(cnt, approx, 0.02 * peri, true);\n
\n
                        // Looking for 4 corners and convexity\n
                        if (approx.rows === 4 && cv.isContourConvex(approx)) {\n
                            if (area > maxArea) {\n
                                maxArea = area;\n
                                bestCandidate = [];\n
                                for(let j=0; j<4; j++) {\n
                                    bestCandidate.push({\n
                                        x: approx.data32S[j*2] / scale,\n
                                        y: approx.data32S[j*2+1] / scale\n
                                    });\n
                                }\n
                            }\n
                        }\n
\n
                        approx.delete();\n
                        approx = null;\n
                    }\n
\n
                    if (bestCandidate) {\n
                        bestCorners = this.sortPoints(bestCandidate);\n
                        isFound = true;\n
                    } else {\n
                        bestCorners = this.getDefaultCorners(width, height);\n
                        isFound = false;\n
                    }\n
\n
                } catch(e) {\n
                    console.error("Detection Logic Error:", e);\n
                    bestCorners = this.getDefaultCorners(width, height);\n
                    isFound = false;\n
                } finally {\n
                    if (approx) approx.delete();\n
                    if (processingSrc && processingSrc !== src) processingSrc.delete();\n
                    if (gray) gray.delete();\n
                    if (blurred) blurred.delete();\n
                    if (edges) edges.delete();\n
                    if (dilated) dilated.delete();\n
                    if (kernel) kernel.delete();\n
                    if (contours) contours.delete();\n
                    if (hierarchy) hierarchy.delete();\n
                }\n
\n
                return { corners: bestCorners, found: isFound };\n
            },\n
\n
            performWarp(src, corners, width, height, filterType = 'original') {\n
                let srcTri = null;\n
                let dstTri = null;\n
                let M = null;\n
                let dst = null;\n
                let finalDst = null;\n
\n
                try {\n
                     // Validate Corners for OpenCV\n
                     const safeCorners = [\n
                        Number(corners[0]?.x) || 0, Number(corners[0]?.y) || 0,\n
                        Number(corners[1]?.x) || 0, Number(corners[1]?.y) || 0,\n
                        Number(corners[2]?.x) || 0, Number(corners[2]?.y) || 0,\n
                        Number(corners[3]?.x) || 0, Number(corners[3]?.y) || 0\n
                     ];\n
\n
                     // Convert corners array to flat array for OpenCV\n
                     srcTri = cv.matFromArray(4, 1, cv.CV_32FC2, safeCorners);\n
\n
                    // Calculate dimensions of the new cropped image\n
                    const wTop = Math.hypot(corners[1].x - corners[0].x, corners[1].y - corners[0].y);\n
                    const wBot = Math.hypot(corners[2].x - corners[3].x, corners[2].y - corners[3].y);\n
                    const hLeft = Math.hypot(corners[3].x - corners[0].x, corners[3].y - corners[0].y);\n
                    const hRight = Math.hypot(corners[2].x - corners[1].x, corners[2].y - corners[1].y);\n
\n
                    let maxWidth = Math.round(Math.max(wTop, wBot));\n
                    let maxHeight = Math.round(Math.max(hLeft, hRight));\n
\n
                    // Validation: Ensure valid dimensions to prevent WASM table index errors\n
                    if (maxWidth < 1) maxWidth = 1;\n
                    if (maxHeight < 1) maxHeight = 1;\n
\n
                    dstTri = cv.matFromArray(4, 1, cv.CV_32FC2, [\n
                        0, 0,\n
                        maxWidth, 0,\n
                        maxWidth, maxHeight,\n
                        0, maxHeight\n
                    ]);\n
\n
                    M = cv.getPerspectiveTransform(srcTri, dstTri);\n
                    dst = new cv.Mat();\n
                    cv.warpPerspective(src, dst, M, new cv.Size(maxWidth, maxHeight), cv.INTER_LINEAR, cv.BORDER_CONSTANT, new cv.Scalar(0, 0, 0, 0));\n
\n
                    // Apply Filter\n
                    if (filterType && filterType !== 'original') {\n
                        finalDst = this.applyFilter(dst, filterType);\n
                    } else {\n
                        finalDst = dst;\n
                    }\n
\n
                    // Draw to temp canvas\n
                    const tempCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n
                    tempCanvas.width = maxWidth;\n
                    tempCanvas.height = maxHeight;\n
                    cv.imshow(tempCanvas, finalDst);\n
                    return tempCanvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.95);\n
\n
                } catch (e) {\n
                    console.error("Warp Error:", e);\n
                    throw e;\n
                } finally {\n
                    if (finalDst && finalDst !== dst) finalDst.delete();\n
                    if (srcTri) srcTri.delete();\n
                    if (dstTri) dstTri.delete();\n
                    if (M) M.delete();\n
                    if (dst) dst.delete();\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            getDefaultCorners(w, h) {\n
                // Default to FULL IMAGE (0,0 to w,h)\n
                // This ensures that if detection fails, we don't crop out important edges.\n
                return [\n
                    {x: 0, y: 0},\n
                    {x: w, y: 0},\n
                    {x: w, y: h},\n
                    {x: 0, y: h}\n
                ];\n
            },\n
\n
            finishCapture() {\n
                this.stopCamera();\n
                this.view = 'review';\n
            },\n
\n
            removeImage(index) {\n
                // Update selection indices before removing\n
                // If removed index is selected, deselect it\n
                // If removed index < selected index, shift selected index down\n
                this.selectedIndices = this.selectedIndices.filter(i => i !== index).map(i => i > index ? i - 1 : i);\n
\n
                this.capturedImages.splice(index, 1);\n
                if(this.capturedImages.length === 0) {\n
                    this.view = 'camera';\n
                    this.startCamera();\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            toggleSelection(index) {\n
                if (this.selectedIndices.includes(index)) {\n
                    this.selectedIndices = this.selectedIndices.filter(i => i !== index);\n
                } else {\n
                    this.selectedIndices.push(index);\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            // --- SORTING LOGIC ---\n
            startSorting() {\n
                this.isSorting = true;\n
                this.sortOrder = [];\n
                this.selectedIndices = []; // Clear layout selection to avoid confusion\n
            },\n
\n
            cancelSorting() {\n
                this.isSorting = false;\n
                this.sortOrder = [];\n
            },\n
\n
            resetSorting() {\n
                this.sortOrder = [];\n
            },\n
\n
            handleImageClick(index, id) {\n
                if (this.isSorting) {\n
                    this.toggleSort(id);\n
                } else {\n
                    this.toggleSelection(index);\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            toggleSort(id) {\n
                const idx = this.sortOrder.indexOf(id);\n
                if (idx === -1) {\n
                    // Add to end\n
                    this.sortOrder.push(id);\n
                } else {\n
                    // Only allow removing if it's the LAST item\n
                    if (idx === this.sortOrder.length - 1) {\n
                        this.sortOrder.pop();\n
                    } else {\n
                        // Optional: Feedback that you can't remove middle items\n
                        // alert("Can only undo the last selection");\n
                    }\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            getSortIndex(id) {\n
                const idx = this.sortOrder.indexOf(id);\n
                return idx !== -1 ? idx + 1 : '';\n
            },\n
\n
            applySorting() {\n
                if (this.sortOrder.length === 0) {\n
                    this.cancelSorting();\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Create new array based on sortOrder\n
                const newImages = [];\n
\n
                // 1. Add sorted items\n
                this.sortOrder.forEach(id => {\n
                    const img = this.capturedImages.find(i => i.id === id);\n
                    if (img) newImages.push(img);\n
                });\n
\n
                // 2. Append remaining items (that weren't selected)\n
                this.capturedImages.forEach(img => {\n
                    if (!this.sortOrder.includes(img.id)) {\n
                        newImages.push(img);\n
                    }\n
                });\n
\n
                this.capturedImages = newImages;\n
                this.cancelSorting();\n
            },\n
\n
            async generateLayout(type) {\n
                this.isLoading = true;\n
                this.loadingMessage = 'Preparing Layout...';\n
\n
                try {\n
                    // 1. Prepare Source Images\n
                    this.layoutSourceImages = [];\n
                    for(const idx of this.selectedIndices) {\n
                        // We store the data URL directly in layoutSourceImages\n
                        this.layoutSourceImages.push({\n
                            src: this.capturedImages[idx].cropped,\n
                            originalIndex: idx\n
                        });\n
                    }\n
                    this.layoutType = type;\n
\n
                    // 2. Decide Flow\n
                    // If multiple images, go to Layout Editor to allow swapping\n
                    // If single image, we can arguably skip editor, but maybe user wants to see the preview (e.g. passport placement)?\n
                    // Let's ALWAYS show editor for consistency, or at least for complex ones.\n
                    // User request: "User must be able to move... in case they want to swap".\n
                    // This implies >1 image.\n
\n
                    if (this.layoutSourceImages.length > 1) {\n
                         this.view = 'layout_editor';\n
                         this.updateLayoutPreview();\n
                    } else {\n
                        // Direct Save for single image (faster workflow)\n
                         const img = await this.loadImage(this.layoutSourceImages[0].src);\n
                         const layoutDataUrl = this.renderLayoutToCanvas(type, [img]);\n
                         this.saveLayoutResult(layoutDataUrl);\n
                    }\n
\n
                } catch(e) {\n
                    console.error("Layout Error", e);\n
                    alert("Error generating layout: " + e.message);\n
                } finally {\n
                    this.isLoading = false;\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            async updateLayoutPreview() {\n
                // Convert source objects to Image elements\n
                const imgs = [];\n
                for(const item of this.layoutSourceImages) {\n
                    const i = await this.loadImage(item.src);\n
                    imgs.push(i);\n
                }\n
\n
                this.layoutPreviewImage = this.renderLayoutToCanvas(this.layoutType, imgs);\n
            },\n
\n
            moveLayoutItem(index, direction) {\n
                const newIndex = index + direction;\n
                if (newIndex < 0 || newIndex >= this.layoutSourceImages.length) return;\n
\n
                // Swap\n
                const temp = this.layoutSourceImages[index];\n
                this.layoutSourceImages[index] = this.layoutSourceImages[newIndex];\n
                this.layoutSourceImages[newIndex] = temp;\n
\n
                this.updateLayoutPreview();\n
            },\n
\n
            cancelLayout() {\n
                this.view = 'review';\n
                this.layoutSourceImages = [];\n
                this.layoutPreviewImage = null;\n
            },\n
\n
            confirmLayout() {\n
                if (this.layoutPreviewImage) {\n
                    this.saveLayoutResult(this.layoutPreviewImage);\n
                    this.cancelLayout(); // Back to review\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            saveLayoutResult(dataUrl) {\n
                this.capturedImages.push({\n
                    id: Date.now(),\n
                    original: dataUrl,\n
                    cropped: dataUrl,\n
                    corners: this.getDefaultCorners(1240, 1754),\n
                    isFound: true\n
                });\n
                // Optional: Scroll to bottom\n
            },\n
\n
            renderLayoutToCanvas(type, images) {\n
                const a4w = 1240;\n
                const a4h = 1754;\n
                const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n
                canvas.width = a4w;\n
                canvas.height = a4h;\n
                const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n
\n
                // Fill White Background\n
                ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n
                ctx.fillRect(0, 0, a4w, a4h);\n
\n
                const margin = 40;\n
\n
                // Helper to draw image fitting within a box\n
                const drawFit = (img, x, y, w, h) => {\n
                    const scale = Math.min(w / img.width, h / img.height);\n
                    const drawW = img.width * scale;\n
                    const drawH = img.height * scale;\n
                    const drawX = x + (w - drawW) / 2;\n
                    const drawY = y + (h - drawH) / 2;\n
                    ctx.drawImage(img, drawX, drawY, drawW, drawH);\n
                };\n
\n
                if (type === 'full') {\n
                    // 1 Image Full Page (with margin)\n
                    if(images[0]) drawFit(images[0], margin, margin, a4w - 2*margin, a4h - 2*margin);\n
                }\n
                else if (type === '70') {\n
                    // 1 Image 70% Scale (Relative to A4 width)\n
                    if(images[0]) {\n
                        const targetW = a4w * 0.7;\n
                        const targetH = a4h * 0.7; // Bound by 70% height too?\n
                        // "70% of page" usually means 70% size.\n
                        drawFit(images[0], (a4w - targetW)/2, (a4h - targetH)/2, targetW, targetH);\n
                    }\n
                }\n
                else if (type === 'passport') {\n
                    // Passport (Spread/Open ID-3): 176mm x 125mm @ 150DPI\n
                    // W: (176 / 25.4) * 150 = 1039 px\n
                    // H: (125 / 25.4) * 150 = 738 px\n
                    const pw = 1039;\n
                    const ph = 738;\n
                    // Center it\n
                    if(images[0]) drawFit(images[0], (a4w - pw)/2, (a4h - ph)/2, pw, ph);\n
                }\n
                else if (type === 'card') {\n
                    // Credit Card (ID-1): 85.6mm x 54mm @ 150DPI\n
                    // W: (85.6 / 25.4) * 150 = 506 px -> +15% = 582 px\n
                    // H: (54 / 25.4) * 150 = 319 px -> +15% = 367 px\n
                    const cw = 582;\n
                    const ch = 367;\n
                    if(images[0]) drawFit(images[0], (a4w - cw)/2, (a4h - ch)/2, cw, ch);\n
                }\n
                else if (type === 'half_v') {\n
                    // Top / Bottom\n
                    const hHalf = a4h / 2;\n
                    if(images[0]) drawFit(images[0], margin, margin, a4w - 2*margin, hHalf - 2*margin);\n
                    if(images[1]) drawFit(images[1], margin, hHalf + margin, a4w - 2*margin, hHalf - 2*margin);\n
\n
                    // Divider Line (Optional - Visual Aid)\n
                    ctx.beginPath();\n
                    ctx.moveTo(0, hHalf);\n
                    ctx.lineTo(a4w, hHalf);\n
                    ctx.strokeStyle = '#e5e7eb';\n
                    ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n
                    // ctx.stroke(); // Maybe don't draw lines on the document itself\n
                }\n
                else if (type === 'half_h') {\n
                    // Left / Right\n
                    const wHalf = a4w / 2;\n
                    if(images[0]) drawFit(images[0], margin, margin, wHalf - 2*margin, a4h - 2*margin);\n
                    if(images[1]) drawFit(images[1], wHalf + margin, margin, wHalf - 2*margin, a4h - 2*margin);\n
                }\n
                else if (type === 'id_card_pair') {\n
                    // Specific ID Card Layout (Center Top / Center Bottom)\n
                    // Standard ID-1 Size: 506 x 319 px -> +15% = 582 x 367 px\n
                    const cardW = 582;\n
                    const cardH = 367;\n
\n
                    const topY = a4h/4 - cardH/2;\n
                    const botY = a4h*3/4 - cardH/2;\n
\n
                    if(images[0]) drawFit(images[0], (a4w - cardW)/2, topY, cardW, cardH);\n
                    if(images[1]) drawFit(images[1], (a4w - cardW)/2, botY, cardW, cardH);\n
                }\n
                else if (type === 'grid') {\n
                    // 2x2 Grid for 3 or 4 images\n
                    // 3 images -> 2 top, 1 bottom center? Or 3 vertical?\n
                    // "3 รูปต่อ 1 หน้า"\n
                    // Common: Top-Left, Top-Right, Bottom-Left, (Bottom-Right)\n
                    const wHalf = a4w / 2;\n
                    const hHalf = a4h / 2;\n
\n
                    // Grid 1 (TL)\n
                    if(images[0]) drawFit(images[0], margin, margin, wHalf - 2*margin, hHalf - 2*margin);\n
                    // Grid 2 (TR)\n
                    if(images[1]) drawFit(images[1], wHalf + margin, margin, wHalf - 2*margin, hHalf - 2*margin);\n
                    // Grid 3 (BL)\n
                    if(images[2]) drawFit(images[2], margin, hHalf + margin, wHalf - 2*margin, hHalf - 2*margin);\n
                    // Grid 4 (BR)\n
                    if(images[3]) drawFit(images[3], wHalf + margin, hHalf + margin, wHalf - 2*margin, hHalf - 2*margin);\n
                }\n
\n
                return canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.9);\n
            },\n
\n
            // --- EDIT / CROP LOGIC ---\n
\n
            startEdit(index) {\n
                this.currentEditIndex = index;\n
                this.view = 'crop';\n
                this.rotation = 0; // Reset rotation\n
                this.activeFilter = 'original'; // Reset filter\n
                const item = this.capturedImages[index];\n
\n
                this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                    this.loadImageForCrop(item.original, item.corners);\n
                });\n
            },\n
\n
            rotateImage(degrees) {\n
                // 1. Get current normalized corners before rotation\n
                const oldW = this.canvasWidth;\n
                const oldH = this.canvasHeight;\n
                const normCorners = this.corners.map(c => ({\n
                    u: c.x / oldW,\n
                    v: c.y / oldH\n
                }));\n
\n
                // 2. Update Rotation\n
                this.rotation = (this.rotation + degrees) % 360;\n
                if(this.rotation < 0) this.rotation += 360;\n
\n
                // 3. Transform Normalized Corners\n
                // 90 deg CW: (u, v) -> (1-v, u)\n
                // -90 deg CCW: (u, v) -> (v, 1-u)\n
                let newNormCorners = [];\n
                if (degrees === 90) {\n
                    newNormCorners = normCorners.map(c => ({ u: 1 - c.v, v: c.u }));\n
                    // FIX: Shift array to maintain TL, TR, BR, BL order\n
                    // Old TL (0) -> New TR. We need New TL to be at index 0.\n
                    // The point that became New TL is Old BL (3).\n
                    // So we move last element to first.\n
                    if (newNormCorners.length === 4) newNormCorners.unshift(newNormCorners.pop());\n
                } else if (degrees === -90) {\n
                    newNormCorners = normCorners.map(c => ({ u: c.v, v: 1 - c.u }));\n
                    // FIX: Shift array to maintain TL, TR, BR, BL order\n
                    // Old TL (0) -> New BL. We need New TL to be at index 0.\n
                    // The point that became New TL is Old TR (1).\n
                    // So we move first element to last.\n
                    if (newNormCorners.length === 4) newNormCorners.push(newNormCorners.shift());\n
                } else {\n
                    newNormCorners = normCorners;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Re-render\n
                const item = this.capturedImages[this.currentEditIndex];\n
                this.loadImageForCrop(item.original, newNormCorners, false, true);\n
            },\n
\n
            loadImageForCrop(src, savedCorners, forceFull = false, isNormalized = false) {\n
                const img = new Image();\n
                img.onload = () => {\n
                    // Handle Rotation Logic (Virtual Canvas)\n
                    // If rotation is not 0, we must draw the image rotated onto a temporary canvas\n
                    // and use that as our source "imageWidth/Height"\n
\n
                    let srcWidth = img.width;\n
                    let srcHeight = img.height;\n
\n
                    // Create an intermediate canvas for rotation if needed\n
                    const rotCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n
                    const rotCtx = rotCanvas.getContext('2d');\n
\n
                    if (this.rotation % 180 !== 0) {\n
                        rotCanvas.width = srcHeight;\n
                        rotCanvas.height = srcWidth;\n
                    } else {\n
                        rotCanvas.width = srcWidth;\n
                        rotCanvas.height = srcHeight;\n
                    }\n
\n
                    rotCtx.translate(rotCanvas.width/2, rotCanvas.height/2);\n
                    rotCtx.rotate(this.rotation * Math.PI / 180);\n
                    rotCtx.drawImage(img, -srcWidth/2, -srcHeight/2);\n
\n
                    // Now use rotCanvas as the source\n
                    this.imageWidth = rotCanvas.width;\n
                    this.imageHeight = rotCanvas.height;\n
\n
                    // SAVE THIS for preview updates\n
                    this.editorSourceCanvas = rotCanvas;\n
\n
                    const canvas = this.$refs.cropCanvas;\n
                    const container = this.$refs.cropContainer;\n
\n
                    // Simple fit logic\n
                    const scale = Math.min(container.clientWidth / this.imageWidth, container.clientHeight / this.imageHeight) * 0.9;\n
\n
                    this.canvasWidth = Math.max(1, Math.floor(this.imageWidth * scale));\n
                    this.canvasHeight = Math.max(1, Math.floor(this.imageHeight * scale));\n
\n
                    canvas.width = this.canvasWidth;\n
                    canvas.height = this.canvasHeight;\n
\n
                    // Render with filter check\n
                    this.renderEditorCanvas();\n
\n
                    this.scaleX = this.canvasWidth / this.imageWidth;\n
                    this.scaleY = this.canvasHeight / this.imageHeight;\n
\n
                    if (forceFull) {\n
                        this.resetToFull();\n
                    } else {\n
                        if (isNormalized) {\n
                            // Map normalized (0..1) to New Canvas Dimensions\n
                            this.corners = savedCorners.map(c => ({\n
                                x: c.u * this.canvasWidth,\n
                                y: c.v * this.canvasHeight\n
                            }));\n
                        } else {\n
                            // Map Original Image Coordinates to Canvas\n
                            this.corners = savedCorners.map(c => ({\n
                                x: c.x * this.scaleX,\n
                                y: c.y * this.scaleY\n
                            }));\n
                        }\n
                    }\n
                };\n
                img.src = src;\n
            },\n
\n
            renderEditorCanvas() {\n
                if (!this.editorSourceCanvas || this.canvasWidth < 1 || this.canvasHeight < 1) return;\n
\n
                const canvas = this.$refs.cropCanvas;\n
                const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });\n
\n
                // 1. Draw Original (Scaled) - effectively resetting the view\n
                ctx.drawImage(this.editorSourceCanvas, 0, 0, this.canvasWidth, this.canvasHeight);\n
\n
                // 2. Apply Filter (if not original)\n
                if (this.activeFilter !== 'original' && typeof cv !== 'undefined' && this.cvLoaded) {\n
                    let srcMat = null;\n
                    let dstMat = null;\n
                    try {\n
                        const imgData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n
                        srcMat = cv.matFromImageData(imgData);\n
                        dstMat = this.applyFilter(srcMat, this.activeFilter);\n
                        cv.imshow(canvas, dstMat);\n
                    } catch (e) {\n
                        console.error("Preview Filter Error:", e);\n
                    } finally {\n
                        if (srcMat) srcMat.delete();\n
                        // applyFilter usually returns a NEW Mat, so we must delete it.\n
                        // If it returned srcMat (unlikely per implementation), we shouldn't double delete.\n
                        // But my applyFilter always returns a new Mat 'dst'.\n
                        if (dstMat && dstMat !== srcMat) dstMat.delete();\n
                    }\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            resetToFull() {\n
                const w = this.canvasWidth;\n
                const h = this.canvasHeight;\n
                // Full image (no padding)\n
                this.corners = [\n
                    {x: 0, y: 0},\n
                    {x: w, y: 0},\n
                    {x: w, y: h},\n
                    {x: 0, y: h}\n
                ];\n
            },\n
\n
            saveCropEdit() {\n
                if (typeof cv === 'undefined') {\n
                    alert("Image Processing Engine (OpenCV) is not loaded. Cannot save edits.");\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
                const item = this.capturedImages[this.currentEditIndex];\n
\n
                try {\n
                    // Fix: Ensure corners are sorted (TL, TR, BR, BL) to prevent twisting\n
                    this.corners = this.sortPoints(this.corners);\n
\n
                    // VALIDATION: Check Scaling Factors\n
                    if (!Number.isFinite(this.scaleX) || this.scaleX <= 0) this.scaleX = 1;\n
                    if (!Number.isFinite(this.scaleY) || this.scaleY <= 0) this.scaleY = 1;\n
\n
                    const realCorners = this.corners.map(c => ({\n
                        x: Number.isFinite(c.x) ? (c.x / this.scaleX) : 0,\n
                        y: Number.isFinite(c.y) ? (c.y / this.scaleY) : 0\n
                    }));\n
\n
                    const img = new Image();\n
                    img.onload = () => {\n
                        let src = null;\n
                        try {\n
                            // Apply Rotation to Source\n
                            const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n
                            // Swap dims if 90/270\n
                            if (this.rotation % 180 !== 0) {\n
                                canvas.width = Math.max(1, img.height);\n
                                canvas.height = Math.max(1, img.width);\n
                            } else {\n
                                canvas.width = Math.max(1, img.width);\n
                                canvas.height = Math.max(1, img.height);\n
                            }\n
\n
                            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n
                            ctx.translate(canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2);\n
                            ctx.rotate(this.rotation * Math.PI / 180);\n
                            ctx.drawImage(img, -img.width/2, -img.height/2);\n
\n
                            // Read from rotated canvas\n
                            const imgData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n
                            src = cv.matFromImageData(imgData);\n
                            const newCroppedUrl = this.performWarp(src, realCorners, canvas.width, canvas.height, this.activeFilter);\n
\n
                            // Create updated object (Deep Copy)\n
                            const updatedItem = {\n
                                ...this.capturedImages[this.currentEditIndex],\n
                                id: Date.now(), // Force reactivity\n
                                cropped: newCroppedUrl,\n
                                corners: realCorners,\n
                                original: canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.9)\n
                            };\n
\n
                            // Update State with Splice to ensure Alpine detects change\n
                            this.capturedImages.splice(this.currentEditIndex, 1, updatedItem);\n
\n
                            // Reset rotation because 'original' is now the rotated image\n
                            this.rotation = 0;\n
                            this.view = 'review';\n
                        } catch (innerError) {\n
                            console.error("Processing Error during Save:", innerError);\n
                            alert("Failed to process image rotation: " + innerError.message);\n
                        } finally {\n
                            if (src) src.delete();\n
                        }\n
                    };\n
                    img.onerror = (err) => {\n
                        console.error("Image Load Error:", err);\n
                        alert("Failed to load source image for processing.");\n
                    };\n
                    img.src = item.original;\n
\n
                } catch(e) {\n
                    console.error("Save Edit Error", e);\n
                    alert("Failed to start save process.");\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            cancelCrop() {\n
                this.view = 'review';\n
            },\n
\n
            // --- FILTERS ---\n
\n
            applyFilter(src, type) {\n
                if (!src || src.isDeleted() || src.empty()) return src;\n
                const dst = new cv.Mat();\n
                try {\n
                    if (type === 'original' || type === 'photo') {\n
                        src.copyTo(dst);\n
                    }\n
                    else if (type === 'scan_doc') {\n
                        // Check image size for kernel\n
                        if (src.cols < 105 || src.rows < 105) {\n
                             src.copyTo(dst);\n
                             return dst;\n
                        }\n
\n
                        // Smart Grayscale\n
                        const gray = new cv.Mat();\n
                        const bg = new cv.Mat();\n
                        const blurred = new cv.Mat();\n
                        try {\n
                            cv.cvtColor(src, gray, cv.COLOR_RGBA2GRAY);\n
\n
                            // 1. Estimate Illumination\n
                            cv.GaussianBlur(gray, bg, new cv.Size(101, 101), 0, 0, cv.BORDER_DEFAULT);\n
\n
                            // 2. Division Normalization\n
                            cv.divide(gray, bg, dst, 255);\n
\n
                            // 3. Sharpen\n
                            cv.GaussianBlur(dst, blurred, new cv.Size(5, 5), 0, 0, cv.BORDER_DEFAULT);\n
                            cv.addWeighted(dst, 1.5, blurred, -0.5, 0, dst);\n
                        } finally {\n
                            gray.delete(); bg.delete(); blurred.delete();\n
                        }\n
                    }\n
                    else if (type === 'high_contrast') {\n
                         const rgb = new cv.Mat();\n
                         const lab = new cv.Mat();\n
                         const planes = new cv.MatVector();\n
                         const mergedPlanes = new cv.MatVector();\n
                         const resultRGB = new cv.Mat();\n
                         let l = null, a = null, b = null, clahe = null;\n
\n
                         try {\n
                             cv.cvtColor(src, rgb, cv.COLOR_RGBA2RGB);\n
                             cv.cvtColor(rgb, lab, cv.COLOR_RGB2Lab);\n
                             cv.split(lab, planes);\n
\n
                             l = planes.get(0);\n
                             a = planes.get(1);\n
                             b = planes.get(2);\n
\n
                             // CLAHE Constructor Check\n
                             if (cv.CLAHE) {\n
                                 clahe = new cv.CLAHE(3.0, new cv.Size(8, 8));\n
                                 clahe.apply(l, l);\n
                             } else {\n
                                 // Fallback if CLAHE missing\n
                                 console.warn("CLAHE not available");\n
                             }\n
\n
                             mergedPlanes.push_back(l);\n
                             mergedPlanes.push_back(a);\n
                             mergedPlanes.push_back(b);\n
\n
                             cv.merge(mergedPlanes, lab);\n
                             cv.cvtColor(lab, resultRGB, cv.COLOR_Lab2RGB);\n
                             cv.cvtColor(resultRGB, dst, cv.COLOR_RGB2RGBA);\n
                         } finally {\n
                             if(rgb) rgb.delete();\n
                             if(lab) lab.delete();\n
                             if(planes) planes.delete();\n
                             if(mergedPlanes) mergedPlanes.delete();\n
                             if(l) l.delete();\n
                             if(a) a.delete();\n
                             if(b) b.delete();\n
                             if(clahe) clahe.delete();\n
                             if(resultRGB) resultRGB.delete();\n
                         }\n
                    }\n
                    else if (type === 'gray') {\n
                        cv.cvtColor(src, dst, cv.COLOR_RGBA2GRAY);\n
                    }\n
                    else if (type === 'bw') {\n
                        const gray = new cv.Mat();\n
                        try {\n
                            cv.cvtColor(src, gray, cv.COLOR_RGBA2GRAY);\n
                            cv.adaptiveThreshold(gray, dst, 255, cv.ADAPTIVE_THRESH_GAUSSIAN_C, cv.THRESH_BINARY, 15, 10);\n
                        } finally {\n
                            gray.delete();\n
                        }\n
                    }\n
                    else if (type === 'magic') {\n
                         if (src.cols < 105 || src.rows < 105) {\n
                             src.copyTo(dst);\n
                             return dst;\n
                         }\n
\n
                         const rgb = new cv.Mat();\n
                         const hsv = new cv.Mat();\n
                         const planes = new cv.MatVector();\n
                         const newPlanes = new cv.MatVector();\n
                         const bg = new cv.Mat();\n
                         const blurredV = new cv.Mat();\n
                         const resultRGB = new cv.Mat();\n
                         let h = null, s = null, v = null;\n
\n
                         try {\n
                             cv.cvtColor(src, rgb, cv.COLOR_RGBA2RGB);\n
                             cv.cvtColor(rgb, hsv, cv.COLOR_RGB2HSV);\n
                             cv.split(hsv, planes);\n
\n
                             h = planes.get(0);\n
                             s = planes.get(1);\n
                             v = planes.get(2);\n
\n
                             // 1. Illumination Normalization\n
                             cv.GaussianBlur(v, bg, new cv.Size(101, 101), 0, 0, cv.BORDER_DEFAULT);\n
                             cv.divide(v, bg, v, 255);\n
\n
                             // 2. Sharpening\n
                             cv.GaussianBlur(v, blurredV, new cv.Size(5, 5), 0, 0, cv.BORDER_DEFAULT);\n
                             cv.addWeighted(v, 1.5, blurredV, -0.5, 0, v);\n
\n
                             newPlanes.push_back(h);\n
                             newPlanes.push_back(s);\n
                             newPlanes.push_back(v);\n
\n
                             cv.merge(newPlanes, hsv);\n
                             cv.cvtColor(hsv, resultRGB, cv.COLOR_HSV2RGB);\n
                             cv.cvtColor(resultRGB, dst, cv.COLOR_RGB2RGBA);\n
                         } finally {\n
                             if(rgb) rgb.delete();\n
                             if(hsv) hsv.delete();\n
                             if(planes) planes.delete();\n
                             if(newPlanes) newPlanes.delete();\n
                             if(bg) bg.delete();\n
                             if(blurredV) blurredV.delete();\n
                             if(resultRGB) resultRGB.delete();\n
                             if(h) h.delete();\n
                             if(s) s.delete();\n
                             if(v) v.delete();\n
                         }\n
                    }\n
                    else {\n
                        src.copyTo(dst);\n
                    }\n
                } catch (e) {\n
                    console.error("Filter Error (" + type + "):", e);\n
                    try {\n
                        if (dst && !dst.isDeleted()) {\n
                             src.copyTo(dst); // Fallback to original\n
                        }\n
                    } catch (err) {\n
                        console.error("Fallback failed:", err);\n
                    }\n
                }\n
                return dst;\n
            },\n
\n
            // --- UTILS ---\n
\n
            sortPoints(points) {\n
                // Sort by Y to separate Top/Bottom pairs\n
                points.sort((a,b) => a.y - b.y);\n
\n
                // Top 2 (TL, TR) - Sort by X\n
                const top = points.slice(0, 2).sort((a,b) => a.x - b.x);\n
\n
                // Bottom 2 (BL, BR) - Sort by X (BL is left, BR is right)\n
                // My performWarp expects [TL, TR, BR, BL] based on the destination mapping.\n
                // Dest: [0,0] (TL), [w,0] (TR), [w,h] (BR), [0,h] (BL)\n
                // So index 2 must be BR (largest X in bottom pair), index 3 must be BL (smallest X in bottom pair).\n
\n
                const bottom = points.slice(2, 4).sort((a,b) => a.x - b.x);\n
\n
                // top[0]=TL, top[1]=TR\n
                // bottom[0]=BL, bottom[1]=BR\n
                // Return Order: TL, TR, BR, BL\n
                return [top[0], top[1], bottom[1], bottom[0]];\n
            },\n
\n
            getPolygonPoints() {\n
                return this.corners.map(c => `${c.x},${c.y}`).join(' ');\n
            },\n
\n
            startDrag(type, index, e) {\n
                e.preventDefault();\n
                e.stopPropagation();\n
\n
                const clientX = e.type.includes('touch') ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX;\n
                const clientY = e.type.includes('touch') ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;\n
\n
                this.previousMousePosition = { x: clientX, y: clientY };\n
\n
                if (type === 'corner') {\n
                    this.activeDragIndex = index;\n
                    this.activeDragEdge = -1;\n
                } else if (type === 'edge') {\n
                    this.activeDragEdge = index;\n
                    this.activeDragIndex = -1;\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            onDrag(e) {\n
                if (this.activeDragIndex === -1 && this.activeDragEdge === -1) return;\n
                e.preventDefault();\n
\n
                const clientX = e.type.includes('touch') ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX;\n
                const clientY = e.type.includes('touch') ? e.touches[0].clientY : e.clientY;\n
\n
                // Calculate Delta\n
                const dx = clientX - this.previousMousePosition.x;\n
                const dy = clientY - this.previousMousePosition.y;\n
\n
                // Update prev position for next frame\n
                this.previousMousePosition = { x: clientX, y: clientY };\n
\n
                // Get Canvas Rect to check bounds relative to canvas\n
                const canvasRect = this.$refs.cropCanvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n
\n
                // Helper to move a point with bounds check\n
                const movePoint = (idx, deltaX, deltaY) => {\n
                     let newX = this.corners[idx].x + deltaX;\n
                     let newY = this.corners[idx].y + deltaY;\n
\n
                     // Clamping\n
                     newX = Math.max(0, Math.min(newX, this.canvasWidth));\n
                     newY = Math.max(0, Math.min(newY, this.canvasHeight));\n
\n
                     this.corners[idx] = { x: newX, y: newY };\n
                };\n
\n
                if (this.activeDragIndex !== -1) {\n
                    // Moving a Corner: Absolute position calculation is smoother for corners than delta,\n
                    // but delta is consistent with edge dragging.\n
                    // Let's switch corner dragging to absolute position like before to prevent drift?\n
                    // Actually, "snap to mouse" is better for corners.\n
\n
                    let x = clientX - canvasRect.left;\n
                    let y = clientY - canvasRect.top;\n
                    x = Math.max(0, Math.min(x, this.canvasWidth));\n
                    y = Math.max(0, Math.min(y, this.canvasHeight));\n
                    this.corners[this.activeDragIndex] = {x, y};\n
\n
                } else if (this.activeDragEdge !== -1) {\n
                    // Moving an Edge: Apply delta to both endpoints\n
                    const edgeIdx = this.activeDragEdge;\n
                    // Edge 0: 0-1, Edge 1: 1-2, Edge 2: 2-3, Edge 3: 3-0\n
                    const p1 = edgeIdx;\n
                    const p2 = (edgeIdx + 1) % 4;\n
\n
                    movePoint(p1, dx, dy);\n
                    movePoint(p2, dx, dy);\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            stopDrag() {\n
                this.activeDragIndex = -1;\n
                this.activeDragEdge = -1;\n
            },\n
\n
            // --- FINALIZATION ---\n
\n
            async finalizeProcess() {\n
                this.isLoading = true;\n
                this.loadingMessage = 'Generating Output...';\n
\n
                try {\n
                    // 1. Validate Target Input\n
                    if (!this.targetInputId) {\n
                        throw new Error("Target input ID is missing.");\n
                    }\n
                    const input = document.getElementById(this.targetInputId);\n
                    if (!input) {\n
                        throw new Error(`Target input element (#${this.targetInputId}) not found.`);\n
                    }\n
\n
                    const dt = new DataTransfer();\n
\n
                    // Standard Logic: Bundle everything into the input\n
                    // If 1 image -> JPG\n
                    // If > 1 image -> PDF\n
                    // Note: If user created a Layout, it is just another image in the list.\n
                    // The user is expected to delete source images if they only want the layout.\n
\n
                    if (this.capturedImages.length === 1) {\n
                        // Check if we should generate PDF for single image to enforce A4 placement\n
                        // For now, we continue to return JPG for single images unless they are specifically from a layout.\n
                        // However, to ensure "Fit to A4" behavior is consistent, we might want to consider PDF.\n
                        // But sticking to JPG for single image is standard for file inputs.\n
                        const file = await this.urlToFile(this.capturedImages[0].cropped, 'scanned_doc.jpg', 'image/jpeg');\n
                        dt.items.add(file);\n
\n
                    } else if (this.capturedImages.length > 1) {\n
                        // Check if jsPDF is loaded\n
                        if (!window.jspdf) {\n
                            throw new Error("PDF generation library (jsPDF) is not loaded. Please check your internet connection.");\n
                        }\n
\n
                        const { jsPDF } = window.jspdf;\n
                        const doc = new jsPDF(); // A4 Portrait by default\n
\n
                        const pageWidth = doc.internal.pageSize.getWidth();\n
                        const pageHeight = doc.internal.pageSize.getHeight();\n
\n
                        for (let i = 0; i < this.capturedImages.length; i++) {\n
                            const imgData = this.capturedImages[i].cropped;\n
                            if (i > 0) doc.addPage();\n
\n
                            const props = doc.getImageProperties(imgData);\n
\n
                            // "Fit to Page" Logic (Aspect Fit)\n
                            // Calculate scale factors for both dimensions\n
                            const scaleW = pageWidth / props.width;\n
                            const scaleH = pageHeight / props.height;\n
\n
                            // Use the smaller scale to ensure the image fits entirely within the page\n
                            const scale = Math.min(scaleW, scaleH);\n
\n
                            const finalW = props.width * scale;\n
                            const finalH = props.height * scale;\n
\n
                            // Center the image on the page\n
                            const x = (pageWidth - finalW) / 2;\n
                            const y = (pageHeight - finalH) / 2;\n
\n
                            doc.addImage(imgData, 'JPEG', x, y, finalW, finalH);\n
                        }\n
\n
                        const pdfBlob = doc.output('blob');\n
                        const file = new File([pdfBlob], "scanned_document.pdf", { type: "application/pdf" });\n
                        dt.items.add(file);\n
                    }\n
\n
                    // Assign to Input\n
                    // Flag the input so the interceptor knows this change comes from the scanner\n
                    input.dataset.scannerSource = 'true';\n
                    input.files = dt.files;\n
                    input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));\n
\n
                    // Handle Preview\n
                    if (this.targetPreviewId && dt.files.length > 0) {\n
                         const preview = document.getElementById(this.targetPreviewId);\n
                         if(preview) {\n
                             const reader = new FileReader();\n
                             reader.onload = (e) => preview.src = e.target.result;\n
                             reader.readAsDataURL(dt.files[0]);\n
                         }\n
                    }\n
\n
                    this.closeScanner();\n
\n
                } catch (e) {\n
                    console.error("Finalize Error", e);\n
                    alert("Failed to save documents: " + e.message);\n
                } finally {\n
                    this.isLoading = false;\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            loadImage(src) {\n
                return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n
                    const img = new Image();\n
                    img.onload = () => resolve(img);\n
                    img.onerror = reject;\n
                    img.src = src;\n
                });\n
            },\n
\n
            async urlToFile(url, filename, mimeType) {\n
                const res = await fetch(url);\n
                const buf = await res.arrayBuffer();\n
                return new File([buf], filename, { type: mimeType });\n
            }\n
        }))\n
    });\n
</script>\n
\n

    <script>\n
    // Global Drag Helper\n
    window.startDragGlobal = function(e, type, data) {\n
        const payload = {\n
            type: type,\n
            title: data.name || data.title || 'Item',\n
            subtitle: data.subtitle || data.code || '',\n
            url: data.url || window.location.href,\n
            source_menu: data.source_menu || document.title, // Default to page title if not provided\n
            ...data\n
        };\n
        e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'copy';\n
        const jsonPayload = JSON.stringify(payload);\n
        e.dataTransfer.setData('application/json', jsonPayload);\n
        e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', jsonPayload); // Fallback for broader compatibility\n
    }\n
\n
    // Register PWA Service Worker & Push Subscription\n
    if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {\n
        window.addEventListener('load', () => {\n
            navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js')\n
                .then(registration => {\n
                    console.log('ServiceWorker registration successful with scope: ', registration.scope);\n
                    initializePushSubscription(registration);\n
                })\n
                .catch(err => {\n
                    console.log('ServiceWorker registration failed: ', err);\n
                });\n
        });\n
    }\n
\n
    // Helper to convert VAPID key\n
    function urlBase64ToUint8Array(base64String) {\n
        const padding = '='.repeat((4 - base64String.length % 4) % 4);\n
        const base64 = (base64String + padding)\n
            .replace(/\-/g, '+')\n
            .replace(/_/g, '/');\n
\n
        const rawData = window.atob(base64);\n
        const outputArray = new Uint8Array(rawData.length);\n
\n
        for (let i = 0; i < rawData.length; ++i) {\n
            outputArray[i] = rawData.charCodeAt(i);\n
        }\n
        return outputArray;\n
    }\n
\n
    function initializePushSubscription(registration) {\n
        // Check if push is supported\n
        if (!('PushManager' in window)) {\n
            console.log('Push messaging isn\'t supported.');\n
            return;\n
        }\n
\n
        // Check permission state\n
        if (Notification.permission === 'denied') {\n
            console.log('The user has blocked notifications.');\n
            return;\n
        }\n
\n
        // We can request permission here, or let the user click a button.\n
        // For PWA experience, we often ask on load or interaction.\n
        // Let's ask on load for now as per requirement "like a modern app".\n
\n
        // Use a dummy VAPID public key if we don't have a real one yet from .env\n
        // In a real scenario, this comes from config.\n
        // I will use a placeholder or check if one is injected.\n
        const vapidPublicKey = 'BCmti7ScwxxVAlB7WAzMMSiwV4-D1_5z509i546e4k7e4k7e4k7e4k7e4k7e4k7e4k7e4k7e4k7e4k7e4k4';\n
\n
        // If key is invalid placeholder, we can't really subscribe securely, but we'll try standard subscribe\n
        // For local testing without VAPID, push might not work fully in all browsers.\n
\n
        // Ideally:\n
        const convertedVapidKey = urlBase64ToUint8Array(vapidPublicKey);\n
\n
        // Check if already subscribed\n
        registration.pushManager.getSubscription()\n
            .then(function(subscription) {\n
                if (subscription) {\n
                    console.log('User is already subscribed:', subscription);\n
                    sendSubscriptionToBackEnd(subscription);\n
                    return subscription;\n
                }\n
\n
                return registration.pushManager.subscribe({\n
                    userVisibleOnly: true,\n
                    applicationServerKey: convertedVapidKey\n
                });\n
            })\n
            .then(function(subscription) {\n
                if (subscription) {\n
                    console.log('User is subscribed:', subscription);\n
                    sendSubscriptionToBackEnd(subscription);\n
                }\n
            })\n
            .catch(function(err) {\n
                console.log('Failed to subscribe the user: ', err);\n
            });\n
    }\n
\n
    function sendSubscriptionToBackEnd(subscription) {\n
        const csrfToken = document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').getAttribute('content');\n
\n
        fetch('http://localhost/push/subscribe', {\n
            method: 'POST',\n
            headers: {\n
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n
                'X-CSRF-TOKEN': csrfToken\n
            },\n
            body: JSON.stringify(subscription)\n
        })\n
        .then(function(response) {\n
            if (!response.ok) {\n
                throw new Error('Bad status code from server.');\n
            }\n
            return response.json();\n
        })\n
        .then(function(responseData) {\n
            if (!responseData.success) {\n
                throw new Error('Bad response from server.');\n
            }\n
        });\n
    }\n
    </script>\n
\n
    <script>\n
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {\n
            const renewModal = document.getElementById('renewNotificationModal');\n
            if (renewModal) {\n
                renewModal.addEventListener('show.bs.modal', function (event) {\n
                    const button = event.relatedTarget;\n
                    const notificationId = button.getAttribute('data-notification-id');\n
                    const notificationType = button.getAttribute('data-notification-type');\n
\n
                    const form = document.getElementById('renew-form');\n
                    const modalTitle = document.getElementById('renewModalLabel');\n
                    const pinkCardGroup = document.getElementById('pink_card_number_group');\n
                    const dueDateGroup = document.getElementById('new_due_date_group');\n

                    const attachmentLabel = document.getElementById('attachment_label');\n
                    const attachmentInput = document.getElementById('attachment');\n
                    const pinkCardInput = document.getElementById('pink_card_number');\n
\n
                    // Reset Fields\n
                    pinkCardGroup.classList.add('d-none');\n
                    dueDateGroup.classList.remove('d-none');\n
                    dueDateInput.required = true;\n
                    pinkCardInput.required = false;\n

                    attachmentLabel.textContent = 'แนบไฟล์เอกสาร (ถ้ามี):';\n
\n
                    if(notificationId) {\n
                        form.action = `/notifications/${notificationId}/renew`;\n
                    }\n
\n
                    // Adjust modal based on type\n
                    if (notificationType === 'pink_card_missing') {\n
                        modalTitle.textContent = 'อัพเดตข้อมูลบัตรชมพู';\n
                        pinkCardGroup.classList.remove('d-none');\n
                        pinkCardInput.required = true;\n

                        dueDateInput.required = false;\n
                        attachmentLabel.innerHTML = 'แนบไฟล์บัตรชมพู <span class="text-danger">*</span>';\n
                        attachmentInput.required = true;\n
                    } else if (notificationType === 'residence_permit_missing') {\n
                        modalTitle.textContent = 'อัพเดตใบแจ้งที่พักอาศัย';\n
                        dueDateGroup.classList.add('d-none');\n
                        dueDateInput.required = false;\n
                        attachmentLabel.innerHTML = 'แนบไฟล์ใบแจ้งที่พักอาศัย <span class="text-danger">*</span>';\n
                        attachmentInput.required = true;\n
                    } else {\n
                        modalTitle.textContent = 'ต่ออายุการแจ้งเตือน';\n
                    }\n
                });\n
            }\n
\n
            const cancelModal = document.getElementById('cancelNotificationModal');\n
            if (cancelModal) {\n
                cancelModal.addEventListener('show.bs.modal', function (event) {\n
                    const button = event.relatedTarget;\n
                    const notificationId = button.getAttribute('data-notification-id');\n
                    const form = document.getElementById('cancel-form');\n
                    if(notificationId) {\n
                        form.action = `/notifications/${notificationId}/cancel`;\n
                    }\n
                });\n
            }\n
        });\n
