    <script>\n
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {\n
        const jobOwnerModalEl = document.getElementById('jobOwnerModal');\n
        if (!jobOwnerModalEl) return;\n
\n
        const jobOwnerModal = new bootstrap.Modal(jobOwnerModalEl);\n
        const jobOwnerList = document.getElementById('jobOwnerList');\n
        const newJobOwnerNameInput = document.getElementById('newJobOwnerName');\n
        const saveNewJobOwnerBtn = document.getElementById('saveNewJobOwnerBtn');\n
        const jobOwnerError = document.getElementById('jobOwnerError');\n
        const mainJobOwnerSelect = document.getElementById('job_owner_id');\n
        const deleteJobOwnerBtn = document.getElementById('deleteJobOwnerBtn');\n
\n
        // --- Main Logic ---\n
\n
        // 1. Open Modal: Fetch and display all current job owners\n
        jobOwnerModalEl.addEventListener('show.bs.modal', function () {\n
            fetchJobOwners();\n
        });\n
\n
        // 2. Add Owner: Handle click on "Save New Owner" button\n
        saveNewJobOwnerBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n
            const name = newJobOwnerNameInput.value.trim();\n
            if (!name) {\n
                showError('กรุณาใส่ชื่อเจ้าของงาน');\n
                return;\n
            }\n
\n
            fetch('http://localhost/job-owners', {\n
                method: 'POST',\n
                headers: {\n
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n
                    'X-CSRF-TOKEN': 'bsHLc7eBpLWbvdc25nzyym5lb7gVho07H9avRWNs',\n
                    'Accept': 'application/json'\n
                },\n
                body: JSON.stringify({ name: name })\n
            })\n
            .then(response => {\n
                if (!response.ok) {\n
                    return response.json().then(err => { throw err; });\n
                }\n
                return response.json();\n
            })\n
            .then(data => {\n
                if (data.success) {\n
                    // Add to modal list\n
                    appendOwnerToList(data.jobOwner);\n
                    // Add to main dropdown and select it\n
                    if (mainJobOwnerSelect) {\n
                        const newOption = new Option(data.jobOwner.name, data.jobOwner.id, true, true);\n
                        mainJobOwnerSelect.add(newOption, null);\n
                    }\n
                    newJobOwnerNameInput.value = '';\n
                    hideError();\n
                }\n
            })\n
            .catch(error => {\n
                if (error.errors && error.errors.name) {\n
                    showError(error.errors.name[0]);\n
                } else {\n
                    showError('เกิดข้อผิดพลาดในการบันทึก');\n
                }\n
            });\n
        });\n
\n
        // 3. Delete Owner: Handle clicks on delete icons in the modal\n
        jobOwnerList.addEventListener('click', function(e) {\n
            if (e.target.classList.contains('delete-job-owner-icon')) {\n
                const ownerId = e.target.dataset.id;\n
                if (confirm('คุณแน่ใจหรือไม่ว่าต้องการลบเจ้าของงานนี้?')) {\n
                    deleteOwner(ownerId);\n
                }\n
            }\n
        });\n
\n
        // 4. Delete selected owner from the main form\n
        if (deleteJobOwnerBtn && mainJobOwnerSelect) {\n
            deleteJobOwnerBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {\n
                const selectedOwnerId = mainJobOwnerSelect.value;\n
                if (selectedOwnerId && selectedOwnerId !== '--- เลือกเจ้าของงาน ---') { // Note: This check might be fragile if translation changes, using ID is better but logic is based on value\n
                     if (confirm('คุณแน่ใจหรือไม่ว่าต้องการลบเจ้าของงานนี้?')) {\n
                        deleteOwner(selectedOwnerId);\n
                    }\n
                } else {\n
                    alert('กรุณาเลือกเจ้าของงานที่ต้องการลบ');\n
                }\n
            });\n
        }\n
\n
\n
        // --- Helper Functions ---\n
\n
        function fetchJobOwners() {\n
            jobOwnerList.innerHTML = '<li class="list-group-item text-muted">กำลังโหลด...</li>';\n
            fetch('http://localhost/job-owners')\n
                .then(response => response.json())\n
                .then(data => {\n
                    jobOwnerList.innerHTML = '';\n
                    if (data.length === 0) {\n
                        jobOwnerList.innerHTML = '<li class="list-group-item text-muted">ไม่มีข้อมูลเจ้าของงาน</li>';\n
                    } else {\n
                        data.forEach(owner => appendOwnerToList(owner));\n
                    }\n
                });\n
        }\n
\n
        function appendOwnerToList(owner) {\n
             // Check if "no data" placeholder exists and remove it\n
            const placeholder = jobOwnerList.querySelector('.text-muted');\n
            if (placeholder) {\n
                placeholder.parentElement.innerHTML = '';\n
            }\n
            const li = document.createElement('li');\n
            li.className = 'list-group-item d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center';\n
            li.id = `job-owner-${owner.id}`;\n
            li.textContent = owner.name;\n
            const deleteIcon = document.createElement('i');\n
            deleteIcon.className = 'bi bi-trash-fill text-danger delete-job-owner-icon';\n
            deleteIcon.style.cursor = 'pointer';\n
            deleteIcon.dataset.id = owner.id;\n
            li.appendChild(deleteIcon);\n
            jobOwnerList.appendChild(li);\n
        }\n
\n
        function deleteOwner(ownerId) {\n
            fetch(`/job-owners/${ownerId}`, {\n
                method: 'DELETE',\n
                headers: {\n
                    'X-CSRF-TOKEN': 'bsHLc7eBpLWbvdc25nzyym5lb7gVho07H9avRWNs',\n
                    'Accept': 'application/json'\n
                }\n
            })\n
            .then(response => response.json())\n
            .then(data => {\n
                if (data.success) {\n
                    // Remove from modal list\n
                    const listItem = document.getElementById(`job-owner-${ownerId}`);\n
                    if (listItem) listItem.remove();\n
\n
                    // Remove from main dropdown\n
                    if (mainJobOwnerSelect) {\n
                        const optionToRemove = mainJobOwnerSelect.querySelector(`option[value='${ownerId}']`);\n
                        if (optionToRemove) optionToRemove.remove();\n
                    }\n
                } else {\n
                    alert(data.message || 'ไม่สามารถลบข้อมูลได้');\n
                }\n
            });\n
        }\n
\n
        function showError(message) {\n
            jobOwnerError.textContent = message;\n
            jobOwnerError.style.display = 'block';\n
            newJobOwnerNameInput.classList.add('is-invalid');\n
        }\n
\n
        function hideError() {\n
            jobOwnerError.textContent = '';\n
            jobOwnerError.style.display = 'none';\n
            newJobOwnerNameInput.classList.remove('is-invalid');\n
        }\n
    });\n
    </script>\n
    <script>\n
    function showToast(message, type = 'success') {\n
        const toastEl = document.getElementById('liveToast');\n
        const toastBody = document.getElementById('toast-body-content');\n
        const toastIcon = toastEl.querySelector('.toast-header i');\n
\n
        // Reset classes\n
        toastIcon.className = 'rounded me-2';\n
\n
        if (type === 'success') {\n
            toastIcon.classList.add('bi', 'bi-check-circle-fill', 'text-success');\n
        } else if (type === 'danger') {\n
            toastIcon.classList.add('bi', 'bi-exclamation-triangle-fill', 'text-danger');\n
        }\n
\n
        toastBody.textContent = message;\n
        const toast = new bootstrap.Toast(toastEl);\n
        toast.show();\n
    }\n
    </script>\n

    <!-- Flatpickr JS -->\n
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/flatpickr"></script>\n
    <script src="https://npmcdn.com/flatpickr/dist/l10n/th.js"></script>\n
\n
    <script>\n
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {\n
        // Function to initialize Flatpickr on a given element\n
        function initFlatpickr(element) {\n
            if (element._flatpickr) return; // Already initialized\n
\n
            // Check if element is readonly or disabled, if so, we might want to respect that\n
            // Flatpickr handles this automatically usually.\n
\n
            flatpickr(element, {\n
                locale: 'th',\n
                dateFormat: 'Y-m-d',\n
                altInput: true,\n
                altFormat: 'd/m/Y',\n
                allowInput: true,\n
                disableMobile: true, // Force Flatpickr UI on mobile for consistency\n
                onChange: function(selectedDates, dateStr, instance) {\n
                    // Dispatch native input event for frameworks like Alpine.js or Vue\n
                    // and 'change' event for standard listeners\n
                    instance.element.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', {bubbles: true}));\n
                    instance.element.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', {bubbles: true}));\n
                }\n
            });\n
        }\n
\n
        // 1. Initialize on existing elements\n
        const dateInputs = document.querySelectorAll('input[type="date"]');\n
        dateInputs.forEach(input => initFlatpickr(input));\n
\n
        // 2. Observe for new elements (e.g. via Alpine or AJAX)\n
        const observer = new MutationObserver(function(mutations) {\n
            mutations.forEach(function(mutation) {\n
                mutation.addedNodes.forEach(function(node) {\n
                    if (node.nodeType === 1) { // Element node\n
                        if (node.tagName === 'INPUT' && node.type === 'date') {\n
                            initFlatpickr(node);\n
                        } else if (node.querySelectorAll) {\n
                             // Check children\n
                            const inputs = node.querySelectorAll('input[type="date"]');\n
                            inputs.forEach(input => initFlatpickr(input));\n
                        }\n
                    }\n
                });\n
            });\n
        });\n
\n
        observer.observe(document.body, {\n
            childList: true,\n
            subtree: true\n
        });\n
    });\n
    </script>\n

<script>\n
    window.backgroundRemoval = {\n
        cache: {\n
            originalFile: null,\n
            transparentBlob: null\n
        },\n
\n
        // Colors\n
        colors: {\n
            'white': '#FFFFFF',\n
            'blue': '#65a5ff' // Standard ID card blue-ish\n
        },\n
\n
        // Main function to process image\n
        async process(file, colorType, onProgress, cancellationToken, skipAi = false) {\n
            // 1. Reset cache if file changed\n
            if (this.cache.originalFile !== file) {\n
                this.cache.originalFile = file;\n
                this.cache.transparentBlob = null;\n
            }\n
\n
            // 2. Handle 'original'\n
            if (colorType === 'original') {\n
                return file;\n
            }\n
\n
            // 3. Get Transparent Blob (Cached or New)\n
            let transparentBlob = this.cache.transparentBlob;\n
\n
            // If we are skipping AI (e.g. manually edited image), treat the file as the transparent source\n
            if (skipAi && !transparentBlob) {\n
                transparentBlob = file;\n
                this.cache.transparentBlob = file;\n
            }\n
\n
            if (!transparentBlob) {\n
                try {\n
                    if (onProgress) onProgress(true, 'Initializing AI Model...');\n
\n
                    // Check if library is loaded (Wait up to 10 seconds)\n
                    const removeBackgroundFn = await this.waitForLibrary(cancellationToken);\n
\n
                    if (cancellationToken && cancellationToken.cancelled) {\n
                        throw new Error('Cancelled by user');\n
                    }\n
\n
                    // --- OPTIMIZATION: Resize large images ---\n
                    // Downscaling to 1200px max dimension significantly speeds up processing (e.g. 10x faster for 12MP photos)\n
                    // while retaining enough quality for ID cards.\n
                    if (onProgress) onProgress(true, 'Optimizing image size...');\n
                    const resizedFile = await this.resizeImage(file, 1200);\n
\n
                    if (onProgress) onProgress(true, 'Removing background (this may take a moment)...');\n
\n
                    // Configuration for better quality and progress tracking\n
                    const config = {\n
                        debug: true,\n
                        device: 'gpu', // Hint to use GPU if available\n
                        progress: (key, current, total) => {\n
                            if (onProgress) {\n
                                const percent = Math.round((current / total) * 100);\n
                                onProgress(true, `Processing: ${percent}%`);\n
                            }\n
                        },\n
                        // model: 'medium', // Default (isnet_fp16) is accurate. We rely on resizing for speed.\n
                        output: {\n
                            format: 'image/png',\n
                            quality: 0.95\n
                        }\n
                    };\n
\n
                    // Execute removal with timeout and cancellation race\n
                    // Use resizedFile instead of original file\n
                    const processPromise = removeBackgroundFn(resizedFile, config);\n
\n
                    const timeoutPromise = new Promise((_, reject) =>\n
                        setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Processing timed out (60s). Please check your connection.')), 60000)\n
                    );\n
\n
                    const cancellationPromise = new Promise((_, reject) => {\n
                        if (cancellationToken) {\n
                            cancellationToken.onCancel = () => reject(new Error('Cancelled by user'));\n
                            if (cancellationToken.cancelled) reject(new Error('Cancelled by user'));\n
                        }\n
                    });\n
\n
                    transparentBlob = await Promise.race([processPromise, timeoutPromise, cancellationPromise]);\n
\n
                    this.cache.transparentBlob = transparentBlob;\n
                } catch (error) {\n
                    console.error('Background removal failed:', error);\n
                    // Provide user-friendly error\n
                    if (error.message === 'Cancelled by user') {\n
                        throw error; // Propagate cancellation\n
                    }\n
                    if (error.message && error.message.includes('fetch')) {\n
                        throw new Error('Failed to download AI model. Please check your internet connection.');\n
                    }\n
                    throw error;\n
                } finally {\n
                    if (onProgress) onProgress(false);\n
                }\n
            }\n
\n
            // 4. Return based on color type\n
            if (colorType === 'transparent') {\n
                return transparentBlob;\n
            }\n
\n
            // 5. Composite for colors\n
            if (onProgress) onProgress(true, 'Applying background color...');\n
            try {\n
                // Check cancellation before compositing\n
                if (cancellationToken && cancellationToken.cancelled) throw new Error('Cancelled by user');\n
\n
                const color = this.colors[colorType] || '#FFFFFF';\n
                return await this.compositeBackground(transparentBlob, color);\n
            } finally {\n
                if (onProgress) onProgress(false);\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        // Helper to resize image\n
        resizeImage(file, maxDimension) {\n
            return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n
                if (!file.type.match(/image.*/)) {\n
                    resolve(file); // Not an image, return original\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                const reader = new FileReader();\n
                reader.onload = (readerEvent) => {\n
                    const image = new Image();\n
                    image.onload = () => {\n
                        let width = image.width;\n
                        let height = image.height;\n
\n
                        if (width <= maxDimension && height <= maxDimension) {\n
                            resolve(file); // No need to resize\n
                            return;\n
                        }\n
\n
                        if (width > height) {\n
                            if (width > maxDimension) {\n
                                height *= maxDimension / width;\n
                                width = maxDimension;\n
                            }\n
                        } else {\n
                            if (height > maxDimension) {\n
                                width *= maxDimension / height;\n
                                height = maxDimension;\n
                            }\n
                        }\n
\n
                        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n
                        canvas.width = width;\n
                        canvas.height = height;\n
                        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n
                        ctx.drawImage(image, 0, 0, width, height);\n
\n
                        canvas.toBlob((blob) => {\n
                            if (!blob) {\n
                                resolve(file); // Fallback\n
                                return;\n
                            }\n
                            resolve(new File([blob], file.name, {\n
                                type: file.type,\n
                                lastModified: Date.now(),\n
                            }));\n
                        }, file.type, 0.95);\n
                    };\n
                    image.onerror = () => resolve(file); // Fallback\n
                    image.src = readerEvent.target.result;\n
                };\n
                reader.onerror = () => resolve(file); // Fallback\n
                reader.readAsDataURL(file);\n
            });\n
        },\n
\n
        // Helper to wait for the ESM module to load\n
        waitForLibrary(cancellationToken) {\n
            return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n
                if (window.imglyRemoveBackground) {\n
                    resolve(window.imglyRemoveBackground);\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                let retries = 0;\n
                const interval = setInterval(() => {\n
                    if (cancellationToken && cancellationToken.cancelled) {\n
                        clearInterval(interval);\n
                        reject(new Error('Cancelled by user'));\n
                        return;\n
                    }\n
                    if (window.imglyRemoveBackground) {\n
                        clearInterval(interval);\n
                        resolve(window.imglyRemoveBackground);\n
                    }\n
                    retries++;\n
                    if (retries > 100) { // 10 seconds\n
                        clearInterval(interval);\n
                        reject(new Error('Background Removal Library failed to load. Please refresh the page.'));\n
                    }\n
                }, 100);\n
            });\n
        },\n
\n
        // Helper to draw blob on colored canvas\n
        compositeBackground(imageBlob, colorHex) {\n
            return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n
                const img = new Image();\n
                img.onload = () => {\n
                    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n
                    canvas.width = img.width;\n
                    canvas.height = img.height;\n
                    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n
\n
                    // Fill background\n
                    ctx.fillStyle = colorHex;\n
                    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n
\n
                    // Draw image\n
                    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);\n
\n
                    // Export as JPEG (since no transparency needed)\n
                    canvas.toBlob((blob) => {\n
                        resolve(blob);\n
                    }, 'image/jpeg', 0.95);\n
                };\n
                img.onerror = reject;\n
                img.src = URL.createObjectURL(imageBlob);\n
            });\n
        }\n
    };\n
</script>\n
    <script>\n
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {\n
    const maxItems = 15;\n
    const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.export-checkbox');\n
    const counter = document.getElementById('selection-counter');\n
\n
    function updateCounter() {\n
        const checkedCount = document.querySelectorAll('.export-checkbox:checked').length;\n
        counter.textContent = `${checkedCount}/${maxItems}`;\n
\n
        if (checkedCount >= maxItems) {\n
            checkboxes.forEach(cb => {\n
                if (!cb.checked) cb.disabled = true;\n
            });\n
            counter.classList.add('text-danger');\n
        } else {\n
            checkboxes.forEach(cb => cb.disabled = false);\n
            counter.classList.remove('text-danger');\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    checkboxes.forEach(cb => {\n
        cb.addEventListener('change', updateCounter);\n
    });\n
\n
    // Reset form when modal closes\n
    const modalEl = document.getElementById('advancedExportModal');\n
    if (modalEl) {\n
        modalEl.addEventListener('hidden.bs.modal', function () {\n
            document.getElementById('advancedExportForm').reset();\n
            updateCounter();\n
        });\n
    }\n
});\n
</script>\n
<script>\n
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {\n
    const searchInput = document.getElementById('target_employer_search');\n
    const resultsContainer = document.getElementById('target_employer_results');\n
    const selectedDisplay = document.getElementById('selected_target_employer_display');\n
    const selectedInput = document.getElementById('selected_target_employer_id');\n
    const confirmBtn = document.getElementById('confirmTargetEmployerBtn');\n
\n
    let debounceTimer;\n
\n
    searchInput.addEventListener('input', function() {\n
        clearTimeout(debounceTimer);\n
        const query = this.value.trim();\n
\n
        if (query.length < 2) {\n
            resultsContainer.style.display = 'none';\n
            return;\n
        }\n
\n
        debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {\n
            fetchEmployers(query);\n
        }, 300);\n
    });\n
\n
    function fetchEmployers(query) {\n
        // Use existing API endpoint or similar\n
        fetch(`http://localhost/api-web/employers/list?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`)\n
            .then(response => response.json())\n
            .then(data => {\n
                resultsContainer.innerHTML = '';\n
                if (data.length > 0) {\n
                    data.forEach(emp => {\n
                        const item = document.createElement('a');\n
                        item.href = '#';\n
                        item.className = 'list-group-item list-group-item-action';\n
                        item.textContent = `${emp.employerNameTh || ''} ${emp.employerNameEn || ''} (${emp.employerId || 'N/A'})`;\n
                        item.dataset.id = emp.id;\n
                        item.dataset.name = `${emp.employerNameTh || ''} ${emp.employerNameEn || ''}`;\n
\n
                        item.addEventListener('click', function(e) {\n
                            e.preventDefault();\n
                            selectEmployer(this.dataset.id, this.dataset.name);\n
                        });\n
\n
                        resultsContainer.appendChild(item);\n
                    });\n
                    resultsContainer.style.display = 'block';\n
                } else {\n
                    resultsContainer.innerHTML = '<div class="list-group-item text-muted">No employers found</div>';\n
                    resultsContainer.style.display = 'block';\n
                }\n
            })\n
            .catch(err => console.error('Error fetching employers:', err));\n
    }\n
\n
    function selectEmployer(id, name) {\n
        selectedInput.value = id;\n
        selectedDisplay.textContent = name;\n
        confirmBtn.disabled = false;\n
        resultsContainer.style.display = 'none';\n
        searchInput.value = ''; // Clear search\n
    }\n
\n
    confirmBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {\n
        const targetEmployerId = selectedInput.value;\n
        const employeeIds = window.pendingTicketEmployeeIds;\n
\n
        if (!targetEmployerId || !employeeIds || employeeIds.length === 0) {\n
            alert('Missing data');\n
            return;\n
        }\n
\n
        // Create form and submit\n
        const form = document.createElement('form');\n
        form.method = 'POST';\n
        form.action = 'http://localhost/employees/bulk-to-ticket';\n
\n
        const csrfToken = document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').getAttribute('content');\n
        const csrfInput = document.createElement('input');\n
        csrfInput.type = 'hidden';\n
        csrfInput.name = '_token';\n
        csrfInput.value = csrfToken;\n
        form.appendChild(csrfInput);\n
\n
        // Add selected Employee IDs\n
        employeeIds.forEach(id => {\n
            const input = document.createElement('input');\n
            input.type = 'hidden';\n
            input.name = 'employee_ids[]';\n
            input.value = id;\n
            form.appendChild(input);\n
        });\n
\n
        // Add Target Employer ID\n
        const empInput = document.createElement('input');\n
        empInput.type = 'hidden';\n
        empInput.name = 'target_employer_id';\n
        empInput.value = targetEmployerId;\n
        form.appendChild(empInput);\n
\n
        document.body.appendChild(form);\n
        form.submit();\n
    });\n
\n
    // Reset modal on close\n
    const modalEl = document.getElementById('selectTargetEmployerModal');\n
    modalEl.addEventListener('hidden.bs.modal', function () {\n
        searchInput.value = '';\n
        selectedInput.value = '';\n
        selectedDisplay.textContent = '-- None Selected --';\n
        confirmBtn.disabled = true;\n
        resultsContainer.style.display = 'none';\n
        // Clean up global variable\n
        window.pendingTicketEmployeeIds = null;\n
    });\n
});\n
</script>\n

<script>\n
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {\n
    let downloadModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('downloadOptionsModal'));\n
    let downloadCenterModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('downloadCenterModal'));\n
    let downloadCenterInterval = null;\n
\n
    // --- 1. Handle Triggering Download Options ---\n
    // From Single Action\n
    document.querySelectorAll('.btn-download-single').forEach(btn => {\n
        btn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {\n
            e.preventDefault();\n
            const empId = this.dataset.employeeId;\n
            document.getElementById('downloadEmployeeIds').value = JSON.stringify([empId]);\n
            downloadModal.show();\n
        });\n
    });\n
\n
    // From Bulk Action (This function should be called by your bulk action handler)\n
    window.openBulkDownloadModal = function(employeeIds) {\n
        if (!employeeIds || employeeIds.length === 0) {\n
            showToast('Please select employees first.', 'danger');\n
            return;\n
        }\n
        document.getElementById('downloadEmployeeIds').value = JSON.stringify(employeeIds);\n
        downloadModal.show();\n
    };\n
\n
    // --- 2. Handle Confirm Download ---\n
    document.getElementById('btnConfirmDownload').addEventListener('click', function() {\n
        const type = document.querySelector('input[name="download_type"]:checked').value;\n
        const files = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('input[name="files[]"]:checked')).map(cb => cb.value);\n
        const empIds = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('downloadEmployeeIds').value || '[]');\n
\n
        if (files.length === 0) {\n
            showToast('Please select at least one file type.', 'danger');\n
            return;\n
        }\n
\n
        const btn = this;\n
        btn.disabled = true;\n
        btn.innerText = 'Starting...';\n
\n
        fetch('http://localhost/admin/downloads/initiate', {\n
            method: 'POST',\n
            headers: {\n
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n
                'X-CSRF-TOKEN': 'bsHLc7eBpLWbvdc25nzyym5lb7gVho07H9avRWNs'\n
            },\n
            body: JSON.stringify({\n
                employee_ids: empIds,\n
                selected_files: files,\n
                type: type\n
            })\n
        })\n
        .then(res => res.json())\n
        .then(data => {\n
            if (data.success) {\n
                downloadModal.hide();\n
                showToast(data.message, 'success');\n
\n
                // Open Download Center to show progress\n
                loadDownloadTasks();\n
                downloadCenterModal.show();\n
\n
                // Auto download if ready (using iframe to avoid popup blockers/replacing page)\n
                if (data.download_url) {\n
                    const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');\n
                    iframe.style.display = 'none';\n
                    iframe.src = data.download_url;\n
                    document.body.appendChild(iframe);\n
                    // Cleanup iframe after a bit\n
                    setTimeout(() => document.body.removeChild(iframe), 60000);\n
                }\n
            } else {\n
                showToast('Error starting download.', 'danger');\n
            }\n
        })\n
        .catch(err => {\n
            console.error(err);\n
            showToast('An unexpected error occurred.', 'danger');\n
        })\n
        .finally(() => {\n
            btn.disabled = false;\n
            btn.innerText = 'Start Download';\n
        });\n
    });\n
\n
    // --- 3. Handle Download Center ---\n
    function loadDownloadTasks() {\n
        const tbody = document.getElementById('downloadTasksTableBody');\n
        // Only show loading if empty, to avoid flickering on refresh\n
        if (!tbody.innerHTML.trim() || tbody.innerText.includes('Loading') || tbody.innerText.includes('No tasks')) {\n
             tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center">Loading...</td></tr>';\n
        }\n
\n
        fetch('http://localhost/admin/downloads/tasks')\n
            .then(res => res.json())\n
            .then(tasks => {\n
                tbody.innerHTML = '';\n
                if (tasks.length === 0) {\n
                    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center">No tasks found.</td></tr>';\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
                tasks.forEach(task => {\n
                    let badgeClass = 'bg-secondary';\n
                    if (task.status === 'completed') badgeClass = 'bg-success';\n
                    else if (task.status === 'processing') badgeClass = 'bg-warning text-dark';\n
                    else if (task.status === 'failed') badgeClass = 'bg-danger';\n
\n
                    let actionBtn = '';\n
                    if (task.status === 'completed') {\n
                        const url = 'http://localhost/admin/downloads/:id/file'.replace(':id', task.id);\n
                        actionBtn = `<a href="${url}" class="btn btn-sm btn-success" target="_blank"><i class="bi bi-download"></i> Download</a>`;\n
                    } else if (task.status === 'failed') {\n
                        actionBtn = `\n
                            <span class="text-danger fw-bold">Failed</span>\n
                            <i class="bi bi-info-circle ms-1 text-muted" data-bs-toggle="tooltip" title="${task.error_message}"></i>\n
                        `;\n
                    } else {\n
                        actionBtn = `<span class="text-muted spinner-border spinner-border-sm" role="status" aria-hidden="true"></span> <span class="text-muted">Processing...</span>`;\n
                    }\n
\n
                    const date = new Date(task.created_at).toLocaleString('th-TH');\n
                    // Pretty type\n
                    let displayType = task.type.toUpperCase();\n
                    if(task.type === 'zip_single') displayType = 'ZIP (Single Folder)';\n
\n
                    tbody.innerHTML += `\n
                        <tr>\n
                            <td>${task.id}</td>\n
                            <td>${displayType}</td>\n
                            <td>${date}</td>\n
                            <td><span class="badge ${badgeClass}">${task.status}</span></td>\n
                            <td>${actionBtn}</td>\n
                        </tr>\n
                    `;\n
                });\n
\n
                // Initialize tooltips\n
                var tooltipTriggerList = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-bs-toggle="tooltip"]'));\n
                var tooltipList = tooltipTriggerList.map(function (tooltipTriggerEl) {\n
                    return new bootstrap.Tooltip(tooltipTriggerEl);\n
                });\n
            })\n
            .catch(err => {\n
                console.error(err);\n
                // Only show error if list is empty, otherwise keep old list\n
                if(tbody.children.length === 1 && tbody.innerText.includes('Loading')) {\n
                     tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-danger">Error loading tasks.</td></tr>';\n
                }\n
            });\n
    }\n
\n
    document.getElementById('btnRefreshDownloads').addEventListener('click', loadDownloadTasks);\n
\n
    // Auto-refresh when modal is open\n
    const centerModalEl = document.getElementById('downloadCenterModal');\n
    centerModalEl.addEventListener('shown.bs.modal', function () {\n
        loadDownloadTasks();\n
        // Poll every 3 seconds\n
        downloadCenterInterval = setInterval(loadDownloadTasks, 3000);\n
    });\n
\n
    centerModalEl.addEventListener('hidden.bs.modal', function () {\n
        if (downloadCenterInterval) {\n
            clearInterval(downloadCenterInterval);\n
            downloadCenterInterval = null;\n
        }\n
    });\n
\n
    // Expose function globally if needed or just rely on button click\n
    window.openDownloadCenter = function() {\n
        loadDownloadTasks();\n
        downloadCenterModal.show();\n
    };\n
\n
    // Listen for a custom event (optional, if you add a navbar link)\n
    document.addEventListener('open-download-center', function() {\n
        window.openDownloadCenter();\n
    });\n
});\n

<script>\n
// Full-Featured Address Management Script\n
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {\n
    // --- Configuration & Global State ---\n
    const thaiDataUrl = "/thai-addresses"; // Hardcoded URL\n
    let thaiAddressData = [];\n
    let dataLoaded = false;\n
    const addressModalEl = document.getElementById('addressModal');\n
    if (!addressModalEl) return; // Exit if the modal isn't on the page\n
\n
    const addressModal = new bootstrap.Modal(addressModalEl);\n
    const addressForm = document.getElementById('addressForm');\n
    const saveBtn = document.getElementById('saveAddressBtn');\n
    const csrfToken = document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').getAttribute('content');\n
    await fetchThaiAddressData();\n
    // --- Form Fields ---\n
    const fields = {\n
        id: document.getElementById('address_id'),\n
        addressable_id: document.getElementById('addressable_id'),\n
        addressable_type: document.getElementById('addressable_type'),\n
        type: document.getElementById('address_type'),\n
        addrNo: document.getElementById('addrNo'),\n
        addrNoEn: document.getElementById('addrNoEn'),\n
        addrMoo: document.getElementById('addrMoo'),\n
        addrMooEn: document.getElementById('addrMooEn'),\n
        addrSoi: document.getElementById('addrSoi'),\n
        addrSoiEn: document.getElementById('addrSoiEn'),\n
        addrRoad: document.getElementById('addrRoad'),\n
        addrRoadEn: document.getElementById('addrRoadEn'),\n
        addrProvince: document.getElementById('addrProvince'),\n
        addrProvinceEn: document.getElementById('addrProvinceEn'),\n
        addrDistrict: document.getElementById('addrDistrict'),\n
        addrDistrictEn: document.getElementById('addrDistrictEn'),\n
        addrSubDistrict: document.getElementById('addrSubDistrict'),\n
        addrSubDistrictEn: document.getElementById('addrSubDistrictEn'),\n
        addrZipCode: document.getElementById('addrZipCode')\n
    };\n
\n
    // --- Data Loading ---\n
    async function fetchThaiAddressData() {\n
        if (dataLoaded) return;\n
        try {\n
            const response = await fetch(thaiDataUrl);\n
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Network response was not ok.');\n
            thaiAddressData = await response.json();\n
            dataLoaded = true;\n
            populateProvinces();\n
        } catch (error) {\n
            console.error('Failed to fetch Thai address data:', error);\n
            showToast('ไม่สามารถโหลดข้อมูลที่อยู่ได้', 'danger');\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    // --- Dropdown Population ---\n
    function populateProvinces() {\n
        fields.addrProvince.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- เลือกจังหวัด --</option>';\n
        const uniqueProvinces = [...new Map(thaiAddressData.map(item => [item['province_th'], item])).values()];\n
        uniqueProvinces.sort((a, b) => a.province_th.localeCompare(b.province_th, 'th'));\n
        uniqueProvinces.forEach(item => {\n
            const option = new Option(item.province_th, item.province_th);\n
            fields.addrProvince.add(option);\n
        });\n
    }\n
\n
    function populateDistricts(province) {\n
        fields.addrDistrict.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- เลือกอำเภอ/เขต --</option>';\n
        fields.addrSubDistrict.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- เลือกตำบล/แขวง --</option>'; // Reset sub-districts\n
        if (!province) return;\n
\n
        const districts = [...new Set(thaiAddressData.filter(d => d.province_th === province).map(d => d.district_th))];\n
        districts.sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'th'));\n
        districts.forEach(district => {\n
            const option = new Option(district, district);\n
            fields.addrDistrict.add(option);\n
        });\n
    }\n
\n
    function populateSubDistricts(province, district) {\n
        fields.addrSubDistrict.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- เลือกตำบล/แขวง --</option>';\n
        if (!province || !district) return;\n
\n
        const subDistricts = thaiAddressData.filter(d => d.province_th === province && d.district_th === district);\n
        subDistricts.sort((a, b) => a.subdistrict_th.localeCompare(b.subdistrict_th, 'th'));\n
        subDistricts.forEach(sub => {\n
            const option = new Option(sub.subdistrict_th, sub.subdistrict_th);\n
            fields.addrSubDistrict.add(option);\n
        });\n
    }\n
\n
    // --- Event Listeners for Dropdowns ---\n
    fields.addrProvince.addEventListener('change', function() {\n
        populateDistricts(this.value);\n
        const selectedData = thaiAddressData.find(d => d.province_th === this.value);\n
        fields.addrProvinceEn.value = selectedData ? selectedData.province_en : '';\n
        fields.addrDistrictEn.value = '';\n
        fields.addrSubDistrictEn.value = '';\n
        fields.addrZipCode.value = '';\n
    });\n
\n
    fields.addrDistrict.addEventListener('change', function() {\n
        populateSubDistricts(fields.addrProvince.value, this.value);\n
        const selectedData = thaiAddressData.find(d => d.province_th === fields.addrProvince.value && d.district_th === this.value);\n
        fields.addrDistrictEn.value = selectedData ? selectedData.district_en : '';\n
        fields.addrSubDistrictEn.value = '';\n
        fields.addrZipCode.value = '';\n
    });\n
\n
    fields.addrSubDistrict.addEventListener('change', function() {\n
        const selectedData = thaiAddressData.find(d =>\n
            d.province_th === fields.addrProvince.value &&\n
            d.district_th === fields.addrDistrict.value &&\n
            d.subdistrict_th === this.value\n
        );\n
        if (selectedData) {\n
            fields.addrSubDistrictEn.value = selectedData.subdistrict_en;\n
            fields.addrZipCode.value = selectedData.zip_code;\n
        }\n
    });\n
\n
    // --- Modal Opening Logic ---\n
    addressModalEl.addEventListener('show.bs.modal', async function(e) {\n
        // await fetchThaiAddressData();\n
        const button = e.relatedTarget;\n
        if (!button) return;\n
\n
        const isAddButton = button.matches('.add-address-btn');\n
        const isEditButton = button.matches('.edit-address-btn');\n
\n
        if (isAddButton) {\n
            addressForm.reset();\n
            fields.id.value = '';\n
            fields.addressable_id.value = button.dataset.addressableId;\n
            fields.addressable_type.value = 'App\\Models\\Employer';\n
            fields.type.value = button.dataset.type;\n
            document.getElementById('addressModalLabel').textContent = 'เพิ่มที่อยู่ใหม่';\n
        } else if (isEditButton) {\n
            const addressId = button.dataset.addressId;\n
            document.getElementById('addressModalLabel').textContent = 'กำลังโหลด...';\n
            try {\n
                const response = await fetch(`/addresses/${addressId}`);\n
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch address data.');\n
                const data = await response.json();\n
\n
                addressForm.reset();\n
                for (const key in data) {\n
                    if (fields[key]) {\n
                       fields[key].value = data[key];\n
                    }\n
                }\n
\n
                populateDistricts(data.addrProvince);\n
                fields.addrDistrict.value = data.addrDistrict;\n
                populateSubDistricts(data.addrProvince, data.addrDistrict);\n
                fields.addrSubDistrict.value = data.addrSubDistrict;\n
\n
                document.getElementById('addressModalLabel').textContent = 'แก้ไขที่อยู่';\n
            } catch (error) {\n
                console.error('Error fetching address for edit:', error);\n
                showToast('ไม่สามารถโหลดข้อมูลที่อยู่เพื่อแก้ไขได้', 'danger');\n
                addressModal.hide();\n
            }\n
        }\n
    });\n
\n
    // --- Save Logic ---\n
    saveBtn.addEventListener('click', async function() {\n
        const formData = new FormData(addressForm);\n
        const addressId = fields.id.value;\n
        const url = addressId ? `/addresses/${addressId}` : '/addresses';\n
        const method = 'POST';\n
\n
        if (addressId) {\n
            formData.append('_method', 'PUT');\n
        }\n
\n
        try {\n
            saveBtn.disabled = true;\n
            saveBtn.innerHTML = `<span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm" role="status" aria-hidden="true"></span> กำลังบันทึก...`;\n
\n
            const response = await fetch(url, {\n
                method: method,\n
                body: formData,\n
                headers: { 'X-CSRF-TOKEN': csrfToken, 'Accept': 'application/json' }\n
            });\n
\n
            const result = await response.json();\n
\n
            if (!response.ok) {\n
                if (response.status === 422 && result.errors) {\n
                     let errorMsg = Object.values(result.errors).flat().join('\\n');\n
                     throw new Error(errorMsg);\n
                }\n
                throw new Error(result.message || 'เกิดข้อผิดพลาดที่ไม่ทราบสาเหตุ');\n
            }\n
\n
            showToast(result.message || 'บันทึกที่อยู่เรียบร้อยแล้ว', 'success');\n
            addressModal.hide();\n
\n
            setTimeout(() => location.reload(), 1500);\n
\n
        } catch (error) {\n
            console.error('Save address error:', error);\n
            showToast(error.message || 'เกิดข้อผิดพลาดในการบันทึก', 'danger');\n
        } finally {\n
            saveBtn.disabled = false;\n
            saveBtn.innerHTML = 'บันทึก';\n
        }\n
    });\n
\n
    addressModalEl.addEventListener('hidden.bs.modal', function () {\n
        addressForm.reset();\n
        populateDistricts('');\n
        document.getElementById('addressModalLabel').textContent = 'เพิ่มที่อยู่ใหม่';\n
    });\n
});\n
</script>\n

<script>\n
// --- Global Selection Manager (Persists across pages/searches) ---\n
const STORAGE_KEY = 'selectedEmployeeData';\n
\n
// Helper: Get all stored data\n
window.getGlobalSelectedData = function() {\n
    const stored = sessionStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);\n
    try {\n
        return stored ? JSON.parse(stored) : [];\n
    } catch (e) {\n
        console.error('Error parsing selectedEmployeeData', e);\n
        return [];\n
    }\n
};\n
\n
// Helper: Get just IDs (compatibility)\n
window.getGlobalSelectedIds = function() {\n
    return window.getGlobalSelectedData().map(item => item.id);\n
};\n
\n
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {\n
    // Stores array of objects: { id: "1", employer_id: "5" }\n
    const selectAllCheckbox = document.getElementById('select-all-checkbox');\n
    const employeeCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.employee-checkbox');\n
    const bulkActionBar = document.querySelector('.bulk-action-bar');\n
    const selectedCountSpan = document.getElementById('selected-count');\n
    const bulkActionButton = bulkActionBar ? bulkActionBar.querySelector('button') : null;\n
\n
    // Helper: Save data\n
    window.setGlobalSelectedData = function(data) {\n
        // Ensure uniqueness by ID\n
        const unique = [];\n
        const map = new Map();\n
        for (const item of data) {\n
            if(!map.has(String(item.id))) {\n
                map.set(String(item.id), true);\n
                unique.push(item);\n
            }\n
        }\n
        sessionStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(unique));\n
        updateUI();\n
    };\n
\n
    // Helper: Add items (accepts array of {id, employer_id})\n
    function addItems(newItems) {\n
        const current = window.getGlobalSelectedData();\n
        // Filter out existing items that are being re-added (to update them with potentially newer data)\n
        const newIds = newItems.map(i => String(i.id));\n
        const currentFiltered = current.filter(i => !newIds.includes(String(i.id)));\n
        const combined = [...currentFiltered, ...newItems];\n
        window.setGlobalSelectedData(combined);\n
    }\n
\n
    // Helper: Remove items by ID\n
    function removeItemsByIds(idsToRemove) {\n
        const current = window.getGlobalSelectedData();\n
        const filtered = current.filter(item => !idsToRemove.includes(String(item.id)));\n
        window.setGlobalSelectedData(filtered);\n
    }\n
\n
    // Clear all selections\n
    window.clearGlobalSelection = function() {\n
        sessionStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);\n
        // Uncheck all visible\n
        document.querySelectorAll('.employee-checkbox').forEach(cb => cb.checked = false);\n
        if(selectAllCheckbox) selectAllCheckbox.checked = false;\n
        updateUI();\n
    };\n
\n
    // UI Updater\n
    function updateUI() {\n
        const allData = window.getGlobalSelectedData();\n
        const count = allData.length;\n
        const allIds = allData.map(item => String(item.id));\n
\n
        if (bulkActionBar) {\n
            if (count > 0) {\n
                bulkActionBar.style.display = 'flex';\n
                if (selectedCountSpan) selectedCountSpan.textContent = count;\n
                if (bulkActionButton) bulkActionButton.disabled = false;\n
            } else {\n
                bulkActionBar.style.display = 'none';\n
                if (bulkActionButton) bulkActionButton.disabled = true;\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        // Sync individual checkboxes\n
        if (employeeCheckboxes) {\n
            employeeCheckboxes.forEach(cb => {\n
                cb.checked = allIds.includes(String(cb.value));\n
            });\n
        }\n
\n
        // Sync "Select All" checkbox state based on VISIBLE items\n
        // If all visible items are in the selected set, check "Select All"\n
        if (selectAllCheckbox && employeeCheckboxes.length > 0) {\n
            const allVisibleSelected = Array.from(employeeCheckboxes).every(cb => allIds.includes(String(cb.value)));\n
            selectAllCheckbox.checked = allVisibleSelected;\n
        } else if (selectAllCheckbox) {\n
            selectAllCheckbox.checked = false;\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    // --- Initialization ---\n
\n
    // Note: We use Event Delegation for the individual checkboxes to support dynamic content (AJAX)\n
    // The 'employeeCheckboxes' variable defined above is static. We will re-query it when needed inside updateUI.\n
\n
    // Helper to extract rich data\n
    function getEmployeeData(cb) {\n
        return {\n
            id: cb.value,\n
            employer_id: cb.dataset.employerId || '',\n
            name_th: cb.dataset.nameTh || '',\n
            name_en: cb.dataset.nameEn || '',\n
            photo: cb.dataset.photo || '',\n
            employer_name: cb.dataset.employerName || ''\n
        };\n
    }\n
\n
    // Expose UI refresh globally (for AJAX loaded content)\n
    window.refreshGlobalSelectionUI = function() {\n
        const savedIds = window.getGlobalSelectedIds();\n
        // Re-query checkboxes to include newly added ones\n
        const currentCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.employee-checkbox');\n
        currentCheckboxes.forEach(cb => {\n
            if (savedIds.includes(String(cb.value))) {\n
                cb.checked = true;\n
            } else {\n
                cb.checked = false; // Ensure unchecked if not in state\n
            }\n
        });\n
        updateUI();\n
    };\n
\n
    // 1. Restore state from storage on load\n
    window.refreshGlobalSelectionUI();\n
\n
    // 2. Handle Individual Checkbox Changes (Event Delegation)\n
    document.body.addEventListener('change', function (e) {\n
        if (e.target.matches('.employee-checkbox')) {\n
            const checkbox = e.target;\n
            const data = getEmployeeData(checkbox);\n
            if (checkbox.checked) {\n
                addItems([data]);\n
            } else {\n
                removeItemsByIds([data.id]);\n
            }\n
        }\n
    });\n
\n
    // 3. Handle "Select All" Checkbox\n
    if (selectAllCheckbox) {\n
        selectAllCheckbox.addEventListener('change', function () {\n
            // Re-query currently visible checkboxes\n
            const currentCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.employee-checkbox');\n
            const visibleItems = Array.from(currentCheckboxes).map(cb => getEmployeeData(cb));\n
            const visibleIds = visibleItems.map(item => item.id);\n
\n
            if (this.checked) {\n
                // Check all visible and add to storage\n
                currentCheckboxes.forEach(cb => cb.checked = true);\n
                addItems(visibleItems);\n
            } else {\n
                // Uncheck all visible and remove from storage\n
                currentCheckboxes.forEach(cb => cb.checked = false);\n
                removeItemsByIds(visibleIds);\n
            }\n
        });\n
    }\n
});\n
</script>\n
\n
<script>\n
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {\n
    document.body.addEventListener('submit', function(e) {\n
        if (e.target.matches('.delete-employee-form')) {\n

            const form = e.target;\n

            Swal.fire({\n

                if (result.isConfirmed) {\n
                    const action = form.getAttribute('action');\n
                    const csrfToken = document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]').getAttribute('content');\n
\n
                    fetch(action, {\n
                        method: 'POST',\n
                        headers: {\n
                            'X-CSRF-TOKEN': csrfToken,\n
                            'Accept': 'application/json'\n
                        },\n
                        body: new FormData(form)\n
                    })\n
                    .then(response => response.json())\n
                    .then(data => {\n
                        if (data.success) {\n
                             window.location.reload();\n
                        } else {\n
                            showToast(data.message || 'เกิดข้อผิดพลาดขณะพยายามลบลูกจ้าง', 'danger');\n
                        }\n
                    })\n
                    .catch(error => {\n
                        console.error('Delete Error:', error);\n
                        showToast('เกิดข้อผิดพลาดทางเครือข่าย โปรดลองอีกครั้ง', 'danger');\n
                    });\n

<script>\n
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {\n
    // --- Scroll to Top Logic ---\n
    const scrollToTopLeft = document.getElementById('scrollToTopLeft');\n
    const scrollToTopRight = document.getElementById('scrollToTopRight');\n
\n
    function checkScroll() {\n
        if (window.scrollY > 200) {\n
            scrollToTopLeft.classList.add('show');\n
            scrollToTopRight.classList.add('show');\n
        } else {\n
            scrollToTopLeft.classList.remove('show');\n
            scrollToTopRight.classList.remove('show');\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    function scrollToTop() {\n
        window.scrollTo({\n
            top: 0,\n
            behavior: 'smooth'\n
        });\n
    }\n
\n
    window.addEventListener('scroll', checkScroll);\n
    scrollToTopLeft.addEventListener('click', scrollToTop);\n
    scrollToTopRight.addEventListener('click', scrollToTop);\n
\n
    // --- Drawer Handle Logic ---\n
    const drawerHandle = document.getElementById('drawer-handle');\n
    const sidebarElement = document.getElementById('sidebar');\n
\n
    if (drawerHandle && sidebarElement) {\n
        // Show handle when scrolled down\n
        window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {\n
            // Check if sidebar is currently open to avoid showing handle over it (though CSS z-index/transform handles visibility mostly)\n
            const isSidebarOpen = sidebarElement.classList.contains('show');\n
\n
            if (window.scrollY > 100 && !isSidebarOpen) {\n
                drawerHandle.classList.add('show');\n
            } else {\n
                drawerHandle.classList.remove('show');\n
            }\n
        });\n
\n
        // Open sidebar on click\n
        drawerHandle.addEventListener('click', function() {\n
            const bsOffcanvas = bootstrap.Offcanvas.getOrCreateInstance(sidebarElement);\n
            bsOffcanvas.show();\n
        });\n
\n
        // Hide handle when sidebar is open\n
        sidebarElement.addEventListener('show.bs.offcanvas', function () {\n
            drawerHandle.classList.remove('show');\n
        });\n
\n
        // Re-check scroll position when sidebar closes\n
        sidebarElement.addEventListener('hidden.bs.offcanvas', function () {\n
            if (window.scrollY > 100) {\n
                drawerHandle.classList.add('show');\n
            }\n
        });\n
    }\n
});\n
</script>\n
\n
<script>\n
    // Global Logout Handler for SweetAlert\n
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {\n
        const logoutBtn = document.getElementById('btn-logout');\n
        if(logoutBtn) {\n
            logoutBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {\n
                e.preventDefault();\n
                Swal.fire({\n
                    title: 'Ready to Leave?',\n
                    text: "Select &quot;Logout&quot; below if you are ready to end your current session.",\n
                    icon: 'question',\n
                    showCancelButton: true,\n
                    confirmButtonColor: '#F97316',\n
                    cancelButtonColor: '#6c757d',\n
                    confirmButtonText: 'ออกจากระบบ',\n
                    cancelButtonText: 'ยกเลิก'\n
                }).then((result) => {\n
                    if (result.isConfirmed) {\n
                        document.getElementById('logout-form').submit();\n
                    }\n
                });\n
            });\n
        }\n
    });\n
\n
    // Global Highlight & Scroll Handler\n
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {\n
        if (window.location.hash) {\n
            // Decouple from execution flow to ensure DOM is fully ready\n
            setTimeout(() => {\n
                const id = window.location.hash.substring(1);\n
                const el = document.getElementById(id);\n
                if (el) {\n
                    el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });\n
                    el.classList.add('highlight');\n
                    setTimeout(() => {\n
                        el.classList.remove('highlight');\n
                    }, 5000);\n
                }\n
            }, 300);\n
        }\n
    });\n
</script>\n
